`core/llm/types.go`:
```go
package llm

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
)

type Usage struct {
	Input, Output, CacheRead, CacheWrite int64
}

// PromptTokens is what clients see as usage.prompt_tokens.
func (u Usage) PromptTokens() int64 { return u.Input + u.CacheRead + u.CacheWrite }
func (u Usage) Tokens() int64       { return u.PromptTokens() + u.Output }

type Prices struct {
	InputMicros, OutputMicros         int64
	CacheReadMicros, CacheWriteMicros *int64
}

type Extra struct {
	VertexProject  string            `json:"vertex_project"`
	VertexLocation string            `json:"vertex_location"`
	Headers        map[string]string `json:"headers"`
}

// Model is the runtime form of a models row with its credential decrypted.
type Model struct {
	ID, Name, Provider, UpstreamModel, APIBase string
	CredentialKind                             string // "", "api_key", "gcp_service_account"
	Secret                                     []byte
	Prices                                     Prices
	Extra                                      Extra
	Created                                    int64
}

// Result is an OpenAI-format response body plus normalized usage.
type Result struct {
	Body  []byte
	Usage Usage
}

type Provider interface {
	Chat(ctx context.Context, m *Model, req *ChatRequest) (*Result, error)
	// ChatStream calls emit with each OpenAI-format chunk JSON (no "data: " prefix).
	// It returns the usage seen so far even when it also returns an error.
	ChatStream(ctx context.Context, m *Model, req *ChatRequest, emit func(chunk []byte) error) (Usage, error)
	Embed(ctx context.Context, m *Model, body []byte) (*Result, error)
}

// UpstreamError is a non-2xx (or unreachable) provider response.
type UpstreamError struct {
	Status  int
	Message string
}

func (e *UpstreamError) Error() string { return fmt.Sprintf("upstream %d: %s", e.Status, e.Message) }

// ErrBadRequest wraps request problems detected before calling upstream (unsupported features etc.).
var ErrBadRequest = errors.New("bad request")
```

`core/llm/cost.go`:
```go
package llm

import "strconv"

// Cost returns nano-USD. Prices are micro-USD per 1M tokens, so
// tokens * micros / 1e6 = micro-USD, and * 1000 = nano-USD → tokens * micros / 1000.
func Cost(u Usage, p Prices) int64 {
	cr, cw := p.InputMicros, p.InputMicros
	if p.CacheReadMicros != nil {
		cr = *p.CacheReadMicros
	}
	if p.CacheWriteMicros != nil {
		cw = *p.CacheWriteMicros
	}
	return (u.Input*p.InputMicros + u.Output*p.OutputMicros + u.CacheRead*cr + u.CacheWrite*cw) / 1000
}

func FormatUSD(nanos int64) string {
	return strconv.FormatFloat(float64(nanos)/1e9, 'f', -1, 64)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `request.go`**

`core/llm/request.go`:
```go
package llm

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
)

type Part struct {
	Type     string // "text" | "image_url"
	Text     string
	ImageURL string
}

type Message struct {
	Role  string
	Parts []Part
}

type ChatRequest struct {
	Raw          map[string]json.RawMessage // original body, forwarded as-is to OpenAI-compatible upstreams
	Model        string
	Stream       bool
	IncludeUsage bool // client asked for stream_options.include_usage
	HasTools     bool
	Messages     []Message
	Temperature  *float64
	TopP         *float64
	MaxTokens    *int
	Stop         []string
}

type wireMessage struct {
	Role      string          `json:"role"`
	Content   json.RawMessage `json:"content"`
	ToolCalls json.RawMessage `json:"tool_calls"`
}

type wirePart struct {
	Type     string `json:"type"`
	Text     string `json:"text"`
	ImageURL struct {
		URL string `json:"url"`
	} `json:"image_url"`
}

func ParseChatRequest(body []byte) (*ChatRequest, error) {
	r := &ChatRequest{}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &r.Raw); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid JSON body: %w", err)
	}
	var w struct {
		Model               string        `json:"model"`
		Stream              bool          `json:"stream"`
		StreamOptions       *struct{ IncludeUsage bool `json:"include_usage"` } `json:"stream_options"`
		Messages            []wireMessage `json:"messages"`
		Temperature         *float64      `json:"temperature"`
		TopP                *float64      `json:"top_p"`
		MaxTokens           *int          `json:"max_tokens"`
		MaxCompletionTokens *int          `json:"max_completion_tokens"`
		Stop                json.RawMessage `json:"stop"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(body, &w); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("invalid request: %w", err)
	}
	if w.Model == "" {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("model is required")
	}
	if len(w.Messages) == 0 {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("messages is required")
	}
	r.Model, r.Stream, r.Temperature, r.TopP = w.Model, w.Stream, w.Temperature, w.TopP
	r.IncludeUsage = w.StreamOptions != nil && w.StreamOptions.IncludeUsage
	r.MaxTokens = w.MaxCompletionTokens
	if r.MaxTokens == nil {
		r.MaxTokens = w.MaxTokens
	}
	_, hasTools := r.Raw["tools"]
	_, hasFuncs := r.Raw["functions"]
	r.HasTools = hasTools || hasFuncs

	if len(w.Stop) > 0 && string(w.Stop) != "null" {
		var one string
		if json.Unmarshal(w.Stop, &one) == nil {
			r.Stop = []string{one}
		} else if err := json.Unmarshal(w.Stop, &r.Stop); err != nil {
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("stop must be a string or array of strings")
		}
	}

	for i, m := range w.Messages {
		if m.Role == "tool" || m.Role == "function" || (len(m.ToolCalls) > 0 && string(m.ToolCalls) != "null") {
			r.HasTools = true
		}
		msg := Message{Role: m.Role}
		switch {
		case len(m.Content) == 0 || string(m.Content) == "null":
		case m.Content[0] == '"':
			var s string
			json.Unmarshal(m.Content, &s)
			msg.Parts = []Part{{Type: "text", Text: s}}
		case m.Content[0] == '[':
			var parts []wirePart
			if err := json.Unmarshal(m.Content, &parts); err != nil {
				return nil, fmt.Errorf("messages[%d].content: %w", i, err)
			}
			for _, p := range parts {
				switch p.Type {
				case "text":
					msg.Parts = append(msg.Parts, Part{Type: "text", Text: p.Text})
				case "image_url":
					msg.Parts = append(msg.Parts, Part{Type: "image_url", ImageURL: p.ImageURL.URL})
				}
			}
		default:
			return nil, fmt.Errorf("messages[%d].content must be a string or array", i)
		}
		r.Messages = append(r.Messages, msg)
	}
	return r, nil
}

// SystemText joins all system/developer message text, for providers with a separate system field.
func (r *ChatRequest) SystemText() string {
	var out string
	for _, m := range r.Messages {
		if m.Role != "system" && m.Role != "developer" {
			continue
		}
		for _, p := range m.Parts {
			if p.Type == "text" {
				if out != "" {
					out += "\n\n"
				}
				out += p.Text
			}
		}
	}
	return out
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `openaifmt.go` and `sse.go`**

`core/llm/openaifmt.go`:
```go
package llm

import (
	"crypto/rand"
	"encoding/hex"
	"encoding/json"
	"time"
)

func OpenAIUsage(u Usage) map[string]any {
	return map[string]any{
		"prompt_tokens":         u.PromptTokens(),
		"completion_tokens":     u.Output,
		"total_tokens":          u.Tokens(),
		"prompt_tokens_details": map[string]any{"cached_tokens": u.CacheRead},
	}
}

func newID() string {
	b := make([]byte, 12)
	rand.Read(b)
	return "chatcmpl-" + hex.EncodeToString(b)
}

// Completion builds a non-streaming chat.completion body for translated providers.
func Completion(model, text, finish string, u Usage) []byte {
	b, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]any{
		"id": newID(), "object": "chat.completion", "created": time.Now().Unix(), "model": model,
		"choices": []any{map[string]any{
			"index": 0, "finish_reason": finish,
			"message": map[string]any{"role": "assistant", "content": text},
		}},
		"usage": OpenAIUsage(u),
	})
	return b
}

// Chunker builds chat.completion.chunk bodies sharing one id/created.
type Chunker struct {
	id, model string
	created   int64
}

func NewChunker(model string) *Chunker {
	return &Chunker{id: newID(), model: model, created: time.Now().Unix()}
}

func (c *Chunker) chunk(choices []any, usage any) []byte {
	m := map[string]any{"id": c.id, "object": "chat.completion.chunk", "created": c.created, "model": c.model, "choices": choices}
	if usage != nil {
		m["usage"] = usage
	}
	b, _ := json.Marshal(m)
	return b
}

func (c *Chunker) Role() []byte {
	return c.chunk([]any{map[string]any{"index": 0, "delta": map[string]any{"role": "assistant", "content": ""}, "finish_reason": nil}}, nil)
}

func (c *Chunker) Text(s string) []byte {
	return c.chunk([]any{map[string]any{"index": 0, "delta": map[string]any{"content": s}, "finish_reason": nil}}, nil)
}

func (c *Chunker) Finish(reason string) []byte {
	return c.chunk([]any{map[string]any{"index": 0, "delta": map[string]any{}, "finish_reason": reason}}, nil)
}

func (c *Chunker) Usage(u Usage) []byte { return c.chunk([]any{}, OpenAIUsage(u)) }
```

`core/llm/sse.go`:
```go
package llm

import (
	"bufio"
	"io"
	"strings"
)

// ReadSSE parses a text/event-stream and calls fn once per event.
func ReadSSE(r io.Reader, fn func(event, data string) error) error {
	sc := bufio.NewScanner(r)
	sc.Buffer(make([]byte, 64*1024), 4*1024*1024)
	var event string
	var data []string
	dispatch := func() error {
		if len(data) == 0 {
			event = ""
			return nil
		}
		err := fn(event, strings.Join(data, "\n"))
		event, data = "", nil
		return err
	}
	for sc.Scan() {
		line := sc.Text()
		switch {
		case line == "":
			if err := dispatch(); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		case strings.HasPrefix(line, ":"):
		case strings.HasPrefix(line, "event:"):
			event = strings.TrimSpace(line[len("event:"):])
		case strings.HasPrefix(line, "data:"):
			data = append(data, strings.TrimPrefix(line[len("data:"):], " "))
		}
	}
	if err := sc.Err(); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return dispatch()
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement `registry.go` and `load.go`**

`core/llm/registry.go`:
```go
package llm

import (
	"sort"
	"sync"
)

// Registry is the in-memory model table. Replace is called on startup and after
// every console write to models or credentials. ponytail: single-instance only;
// multiple replicas would need a reload signal (e.g. Postgres LISTEN/NOTIFY).
type Registry struct {
	mu        sync.RWMutex
	byName    map[string]*Model
	providers map[string]Provider
}

func NewRegistry(providers map[string]Provider) *Registry {
	return &Registry{byName: map[string]*Model{}, providers: providers}
}

func (r *Registry) Replace(models []*Model) {
	m := make(map[string]*Model, len(models))
	for _, x := range models {
		m[x.Name] = x
	}
	r.mu.Lock()
	r.byName = m
	r.mu.Unlock()
}

func (r *Registry) Get(name string) (*Model, Provider, bool) {
	r.mu.RLock()
	defer r.mu.RUnlock()
	m, ok := r.byName[name]
	if !ok {
		return nil, nil, false
	}
	p, ok := r.providers[m.Provider]
	return m, p, ok
}

func (r *Registry) List() []*Model {
	r.mu.RLock()
	out := make([]*Model, 0, len(r.byName))
	for _, m := range r.byName {
		out = append(out, m)
	}
	r.mu.RUnlock()
	sort.Slice(out, func(i, j int) bool { return out[i].Name < out[j].Name })
	return out
}
```

`core/llm/load.go`:
```go
package llm

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"log/slog"

	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
	"tollgate/core/vault"
)

func LoadModels(ctx context.Context, store *repo.Store, v *vault.Vault) ([]*Model, error) {
	rows, err := store.ListModels(ctx)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	creds := map[string]*repo.Credential{}
	secrets := map[string][]byte{}
	var out []*Model
	for _, r := range rows {
		if !r.Enabled {
			continue
		}
		m := &Model{ID: r.ID, Name: r.Name, Provider: r.Provider, UpstreamModel: r.UpstreamModel, APIBase: r.APIBase,
			Created: r.CreatedAt.Unix(),
			Prices: Prices{InputMicros: r.PriceInputMicros, OutputMicros: r.PriceOutputMicros,
				CacheReadMicros: r.PriceCacheReadMicros, CacheWriteMicros: r.PriceCacheWriteMicros}}
		if len(r.Extra) > 0 {
			if err := json.Unmarshal(r.Extra, &m.Extra); err != nil {
				slog.Warn("skipping model with invalid extra", "model", r.Name, "err", err)
				continue
			}
		}
		if r.CredentialID != nil {
			id := *r.CredentialID
			if _, ok := creds[id]; !ok {
				c, err := store.GetCredential(ctx, id)
				if err != nil {
					return nil, err
				}
				creds[id] = c
				if pt, err := v.Open(c.Name, c.Nonce, c.Ciphertext); err == nil {
					secrets[id] = pt
				} else {
					slog.Warn("credential failed to decrypt (wrong ENCRYPTION_KEY?)", "credential", c.Name)
				}
			}
			secret, ok := secrets[id]
			if !ok {
				slog.Warn("skipping model: credential unavailable", "model", r.Name)
				continue
			}
			m.Secret, m.CredentialKind = secret, creds[id].Kind
		}
		out = append(out, m)
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

`core/llm/providers/common.go`:
```go
// Package providers adapts upstream LLM APIs to the OpenAI wire format.
package providers

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"io"
	"net/http"

	"tollgate/core/llm"
)

func post(ctx context.Context, c *http.Client, url string, headers map[string]string, body []byte) (*http.Response, error) {
	req, err := http.NewRequestWithContext(ctx, http.MethodPost, url, bytes.NewReader(body))
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	for k, v := range headers {
		req.Header.Set(k, v)
	}
	resp, err := c.Do(req)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, &llm.UpstreamError{Status: http.StatusBadGateway, Message: err.Error()}
	}
	if resp.StatusCode >= 300 {
		defer resp.Body.Close()
		b, _ := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(resp.Body, 64*1024))
		return nil, &llm.UpstreamError{Status: resp.StatusCode, Message: upstreamMessage(b)}
	}
	return resp, nil
}

// upstreamMessage pulls error.message out of OpenAI/Anthropic/Google error bodies,
// falling back to a truncated raw body.
func upstreamMessage(b []byte) string {
	var e struct {
		Error struct {
			Message string `json:"message"`
		} `json:"error"`
	}
	if json.Unmarshal(b, &e) == nil && e.Error.Message != "" {
		return e.Error.Message
	}
	if len(b) > 500 {
		b = b[:500]
	}
	return string(b)
}

func usageFromOpenAI(raw json.RawMessage) llm.Usage {
	var u struct {
		PromptTokens     int64 `json:"prompt_tokens"`
		CompletionTokens int64 `json:"completion_tokens"`
		Details          struct {
			CachedTokens int64 `json:"cached_tokens"`
		} `json:"prompt_tokens_details"`
	}
	json.Unmarshal(raw, &u)
	return llm.Usage{Input: u.PromptTokens - u.Details.CachedTokens, CacheRead: u.Details.CachedTokens, Output: u.CompletionTokens}
}
```

`core/llm/providers/openai.go`:
```go
package providers

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"strings"

	"tollgate/core/llm"
)

type OpenAI struct {
	HTTP *http.Client
}

func (p *OpenAI) url(m *llm.Model, path string) string {
	base := m.APIBase
	if base == "" {
		base = "https://api.openai.com/v1"
	}
	return strings.TrimRight(base, "/") + path
}

func (p *OpenAI) headers(m *llm.Model) map[string]string {
	h := map[string]string{}
	for k, v := range m.Extra.Headers {
		h[k] = v
	}
	if len(m.Secret) > 0 {
		h["Authorization"] = "Bearer " + string(m.Secret)
	}
	return h
}

// body forwards the client's JSON untouched except for model (and stream_options when streaming).
func body(raw map[string]json.RawMessage, upstreamModel string, stream bool) ([]byte, error) {
	out := make(map[string]json.RawMessage, len(raw)+1)
	for k, v := range raw {
		out[k] = v
	}
	out["model"], _ = json.Marshal(upstreamModel)
	if stream {
		so := map[string]any{}
		json.Unmarshal(out["stream_options"], &so)
		so["include_usage"] = true
		out["stream_options"], _ = json.Marshal(so)
	}
	return json.Marshal(out)
}

func (p *OpenAI) Chat(ctx context.Context, m *llm.Model, req *llm.ChatRequest) (*llm.Result, error) {
	b, err := body(req.Raw, m.UpstreamModel, false)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	resp, err := post(ctx, p.HTTP, p.url(m, "/chat/completions"), p.headers(m), b)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	out, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, &llm.UpstreamError{Status: http.StatusBadGateway, Message: err.Error()}
	}
	var env struct {
		Usage json.RawMessage `json:"usage"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(out, &env); err != nil {
		return nil, &llm.UpstreamError{Status: http.StatusBadGateway, Message: "upstream returned non-JSON body"}
	}
	return &llm.Result{Body: out, Usage: usageFromOpenAI(env.Usage)}, nil
}

func (p *OpenAI) ChatStream(ctx context.Context, m *llm.Model, req *llm.ChatRequest, emit func([]byte) error) (llm.Usage, error) {
	var u llm.Usage
	b, err := body(req.Raw, m.UpstreamModel, true)
	if err != nil {
		return u, err
	}
	resp, err := post(ctx, p.HTTP, p.url(m, "/chat/completions"), p.headers(m), b)
	if err != nil {
		return u, err
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	err = llm.ReadSSE(resp.Body, func(_, data string) error {
		if data == "[DONE]" {
			return nil
		}
		var c struct {
			Choices []json.RawMessage `json:"choices"`
			Usage   json.RawMessage   `json:"usage"`
		}
		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(data), &c); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("bad upstream chunk: %w", err)
		}
		if len(c.Usage) > 0 && string(c.Usage) != "null" {
			u = usageFromOpenAI(c.Usage)
			if len(c.Choices) == 0 && !req.IncludeUsage {
				return nil // we injected include_usage; the client didn't ask for this chunk
			}
		}
		return emit([]byte(data))
	})
	return u, err
}

func (p *OpenAI) Embed(ctx context.Context, m *llm.Model, raw []byte) (*llm.Result, error) {
	var in map[string]json.RawMessage
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &in); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: invalid JSON", llm.ErrBadRequest)
	}
	b, _ := body(in, m.UpstreamModel, false)
	resp, err := post(ctx, p.HTTP, p.url(m, "/embeddings"), p.headers(m), b)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	out, err := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, &llm.UpstreamError{Status: http.StatusBadGateway, Message: err.Error()}
	}
	var env struct {
		Usage struct {
			PromptTokens int64 `json:"prompt_tokens"`
		} `json:"usage"`
	}
	json.Unmarshal(out, &env)
	return &llm.Result{Body: out, Usage: llm.Usage{Input: env.Usage.PromptTokens}}, nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`core/llm/providers/anthropic.go`:
```go
package providers

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"strings"

	"tollgate/core/llm"
)

type Anthropic struct {
	HTTP *http.Client
}

const anthropicDefaultMaxTokens = 4096

func splitDataURL(u string) (mime, data string, ok bool) {
	rest, found := strings.CutPrefix(u, "data:")
	if !found {
		return "", "", false
	}
	meta, data, found := strings.Cut(rest, ",")
	mime, isB64 := strings.CutSuffix(meta, ";base64")
	return mime, data, found && isB64
}

func anthropicBody(m *llm.Model, req *llm.ChatRequest, stream bool) ([]byte, error) {
	if req.HasTools {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: tool calling is not supported for anthropic models yet", llm.ErrBadRequest)
	}
	var msgs []map[string]any
	for _, msg := range req.Messages {
		if msg.Role == "system" || msg.Role == "developer" {
			continue
		}
		var content []map[string]any
		for _, p := range msg.Parts {
			switch p.Type {
			case "text":
				content = append(content, map[string]any{"type": "text", "text": p.Text})
			case "image_url":
				if mime, data, ok := splitDataURL(p.ImageURL); ok {
					content = append(content, map[string]any{"type": "image", "source": map[string]any{"type": "base64", "media_type": mime, "data": data}})
				} else {
					content = append(content, map[string]any{"type": "image", "source": map[string]any{"type": "url", "url": p.ImageURL}})
				}
			}
		}
		if len(content) == 0 {
			continue
		}
		msgs = append(msgs, map[string]any{"role": msg.Role, "content": content})
	}
	b := map[string]any{"model": m.UpstreamModel, "messages": msgs, "max_tokens": anthropicDefaultMaxTokens}
	if req.MaxTokens != nil {
		b["max_tokens"] = *req.MaxTokens
	}
	if s := req.SystemText(); s != "" {
		b["system"] = s
	}
	if req.Temperature != nil {
		b["temperature"] = *req.Temperature
	}
	if req.TopP != nil {
		b["top_p"] = *req.TopP
	}
	if len(req.Stop) > 0 {
		b["stop_sequences"] = req.Stop
	}
	if stream {
		b["stream"] = true
	}
	return json.Marshal(b)
}

func (p *Anthropic) send(ctx context.Context, m *llm.Model, body []byte) (*http.Response, error) {
	base := m.APIBase
	if base == "" {
		base = "https://api.anthropic.com"
	}
	h := map[string]string{"x-api-key": string(m.Secret), "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01"}
	for k, v := range m.Extra.Headers {
		h[k] = v
	}
	return post(ctx, p.HTTP, strings.TrimRight(base, "/")+"/v1/messages", h, body)
}

type anthropicUsage struct {
	InputTokens              int64 `json:"input_tokens"`
	OutputTokens             int64 `json:"output_tokens"`
	CacheReadInputTokens     int64 `json:"cache_read_input_tokens"`
	CacheCreationInputTokens int64 `json:"cache_creation_input_tokens"`
}

func (a anthropicUsage) usage() llm.Usage {
	return llm.Usage{Input: a.InputTokens, Output: a.OutputTokens, CacheRead: a.CacheReadInputTokens, CacheWrite: a.CacheCreationInputTokens}
}

func anthropicFinish(stop string) string {
	if stop == "max_tokens" {
		return "length"
	}
	return "stop"
}

func (p *Anthropic) Chat(ctx context.Context, m *llm.Model, req *llm.ChatRequest) (*llm.Result, error) {
	body, err := anthropicBody(m, req, false)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	resp, err := p.send(ctx, m, body)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	raw, _ := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
	var r struct {
		Content []struct {
			Type, Text string
		} `json:"content"`
		StopReason string         `json:"stop_reason"`
		Usage      anthropicUsage `json:"usage"`
	}
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &r); err != nil {
		return nil, &llm.UpstreamError{Status: http.StatusBadGateway, Message: "upstream returned non-JSON body"}
	}
	var text strings.Builder
	for _, c := range r.Content {
		if c.Type == "text" {
			text.WriteString(c.Text)
		}
	}
	u := r.Usage.usage()
	return &llm.Result{Body: llm.Completion(m.Name, text.String(), anthropicFinish(r.StopReason), u), Usage: u}, nil
}

func (p *Anthropic) ChatStream(ctx context.Context, m *llm.Model, req *llm.ChatRequest, emit func([]byte) error) (llm.Usage, error) {
	var u llm.Usage
	body, err := anthropicBody(m, req, true)
	if err != nil {
		return u, err
	}
	resp, err := p.send(ctx, m, body)
	if err != nil {
		return u, err
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	ch := llm.NewChunker(m.Name)
	err = llm.ReadSSE(resp.Body, func(_, data string) error {
		var ev struct {
			Type    string `json:"type"`
			Message struct {
				Usage anthropicUsage `json:"usage"`
			} `json:"message"`
			Delta struct {
				Type       string `json:"type"`
				Text       string `json:"text"`
				StopReason string `json:"stop_reason"`
			} `json:"delta"`
			Usage anthropicUsage `json:"usage"`
			Error struct {
				Message string `json:"message"`
			} `json:"error"`
		}
		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(data), &ev); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("bad upstream event: %w", err)
		}
		switch ev.Type {
		case "message_start":
			u = ev.Message.Usage.usage()
			return emit(ch.Role())
		case "content_block_delta":
			if ev.Delta.Type == "text_delta" {
				return emit(ch.Text(ev.Delta.Text))
			}
		case "message_delta":
			u.Output = ev.Usage.OutputTokens // cumulative
			if ev.Delta.StopReason != "" {
				return emit(ch.Finish(anthropicFinish(ev.Delta.StopReason)))
			}
		case "error":
			return &llm.UpstreamError{Status: http.StatusBadGateway, Message: ev.Error.Message}
		}
		return nil
	})
	if err == nil && req.IncludeUsage {
		err = emit(ch.Usage(u))
	}
	return u, err
}

func (p *Anthropic) Embed(context.Context, *llm.Model, []byte) (*llm.Result, error) {
	return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: embeddings are only supported for openai-compatible models", llm.ErrBadRequest)
}
```

`core/llm/providers/google.go`:
```go
package providers

import (
	"context"
	"crypto/sha256"
	"encoding/json"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"strings"
	"sync"

	"golang.org/x/oauth2"
	"golang.org/x/oauth2/google"

	"tollgate/core/llm"
)

type Google struct {
	HTTP     *http.Client
	TokenFor func(ctx context.Context, serviceAccountJSON []byte) (string, error)
}

func NewGoogle(c *http.Client) *Google {
	var mu sync.Mutex
	sources := map[[32]byte]oauth2.TokenSource{}
	return &Google{HTTP: c, TokenFor: func(ctx context.Context, sa []byte) (string, error) {
		key := sha256.Sum256(sa)
		mu.Lock()
		ts, ok := sources[key]
		if !ok {
			// See Task 10 Step 1 about CredentialsFromJSONWithType vs CredentialsFromJSON.
			creds, err := google.CredentialsFromJSONWithType(context.Background(), sa, google.ServiceAccount, "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/cloud-platform")
			if err != nil {
				mu.Unlock()
				return "", fmt.Errorf("service account: %w", err)
			}
			ts = creds.TokenSource // already caches and refreshes
			sources[key] = ts
		}
		mu.Unlock()
		tok, err := ts.Token()
		if err != nil {
			return "", err
		}
		return tok.AccessToken, nil
	}}
}

func (g *Google) endpoint(ctx context.Context, m *llm.Model, stream bool) (string, map[string]string, error) {
	method, query := "generateContent", ""
	if stream {
		method, query = "streamGenerateContent", "?alt=sse"
	}
	h := map[string]string{}
	for k, v := range m.Extra.Headers {
		h[k] = v
	}
	if m.CredentialKind == "gcp_service_account" {
		if m.Extra.VertexProject == "" {
			return "", nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: model %s needs extra.vertex_project", llm.ErrBadRequest, m.Name)
		}
		loc := m.Extra.VertexLocation
		if loc == "" {
			loc = "us-central1"
		}
		base := m.APIBase
		if base == "" {
			host := loc + "-aiplatform.googleapis.com"
			if loc == "global" {
				host = "aiplatform.googleapis.com"
			}
			base = "https://" + host
		}
		tok, err := g.TokenFor(ctx, m.Secret)
		if err != nil {
			return "", nil, &llm.UpstreamError{Status: http.StatusBadGateway, Message: err.Error()}
		}
		h["Authorization"] = "Bearer " + tok
		return fmt.Sprintf("%s/v1/projects/%s/locations/%s/publishers/google/models/%s:%s%s",
			strings.TrimRight(base, "/"), m.Extra.VertexProject, loc, m.UpstreamModel, method, query), h, nil
	}
	base := m.APIBase
	if base == "" {
		base = "https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta"
	}
	h["x-goog-api-key"] = string(m.Secret)
	return fmt.Sprintf("%s/models/%s:%s%s", strings.TrimRight(base, "/"), m.UpstreamModel, method, query), h, nil
}

func googleBody(req *llm.ChatRequest) ([]byte, error) {
	if req.HasTools {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: tool calling is not supported for google models yet", llm.ErrBadRequest)
	}
	var contents []map[string]any
	for _, msg := range req.Messages {
		if msg.Role == "system" || msg.Role == "developer" {
			continue
		}
		role := "user"
		if msg.Role == "assistant" {
			role = "model"
		}
		var parts []map[string]any
		for _, p := range msg.Parts {
			switch p.Type {
			case "text":
				parts = append(parts, map[string]any{"text": p.Text})
			case "image_url":
				mime, data, ok := splitDataURL(p.ImageURL)
				if !ok {
					return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: google models only accept data: image URLs", llm.ErrBadRequest)
				}
				parts = append(parts, map[string]any{"inlineData": map[string]any{"mimeType": mime, "data": data}})
			}
		}
		if len(parts) > 0 {
			contents = append(contents, map[string]any{"role": role, "parts": parts})
		}
	}
	b := map[string]any{"contents": contents}
	if s := req.SystemText(); s != "" {
		b["systemInstruction"] = map[string]any{"parts": []any{map[string]any{"text": s}}}
	}
	gc := map[string]any{}
	if req.Temperature != nil {
		gc["temperature"] = *req.Temperature
	}
	if req.TopP != nil {
		gc["topP"] = *req.TopP
	}
	if req.MaxTokens != nil {
		gc["maxOutputTokens"] = *req.MaxTokens
	}
	if len(req.Stop) > 0 {
		gc["stopSequences"] = req.Stop
	}
	if len(gc) > 0 {
		b["generationConfig"] = gc
	}
	return json.Marshal(b)
}

type googleResp struct {
	Candidates []struct {
		Content struct {
			Parts []struct {
				Text string `json:"text"`
			} `json:"parts"`
		} `json:"content"`
		FinishReason string `json:"finishReason"`
	} `json:"candidates"`
	UsageMetadata struct {
		PromptTokenCount        int64 `json:"promptTokenCount"`
		CachedContentTokenCount int64 `json:"cachedContentTokenCount"`
		CandidatesTokenCount    int64 `json:"candidatesTokenCount"`
		ThoughtsTokenCount      int64 `json:"thoughtsTokenCount"`
	} `json:"usageMetadata"`
}

func (r *googleResp) usage() llm.Usage {
	u := r.UsageMetadata
	// Thinking tokens are billed as output.
	return llm.Usage{Input: u.PromptTokenCount - u.CachedContentTokenCount, CacheRead: u.CachedContentTokenCount, Output: u.CandidatesTokenCount + u.ThoughtsTokenCount}
}

func (r *googleResp) text() string {
	var b strings.Builder
	if len(r.Candidates) > 0 {
		for _, p := range r.Candidates[0].Content.Parts {
			b.WriteString(p.Text)
		}
	}
	return b.String()
}

func googleFinish(reason string) string {
	switch reason {
	case "":
		return ""
	case "MAX_TOKENS":
		return "length"
	case "SAFETY", "RECITATION", "BLOCKLIST", "PROHIBITED_CONTENT", "SPII":
		return "content_filter"
	}
	return "stop"
}

func (g *Google) Chat(ctx context.Context, m *llm.Model, req *llm.ChatRequest) (*llm.Result, error) {
	body, err := googleBody(req)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	url, h, err := g.endpoint(ctx, m, false)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	resp, err := post(ctx, g.HTTP, url, h, body)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	raw, _ := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
	var r googleResp
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &r); err != nil {
		return nil, &llm.UpstreamError{Status: http.StatusBadGateway, Message: "upstream returned non-JSON body"}
	}
	finish := "stop"
	if len(r.Candidates) > 0 {
		finish = googleFinish(r.Candidates[0].FinishReason)
	}
	u := r.usage()
	return &llm.Result{Body: llm.Completion(m.Name, r.text(), finish, u), Usage: u}, nil
}

func (g *Google) ChatStream(ctx context.Context, m *llm.Model, req *llm.ChatRequest, emit func([]byte) error) (llm.Usage, error) {
	var u llm.Usage
	body, err := googleBody(req)
	if err != nil {
		return u, err
	}
	url, h, err := g.endpoint(ctx, m, true)
	if err != nil {
		return u, err
	}
	resp, err := post(ctx, g.HTTP, url, h, body)
	if err != nil {
		return u, err
	}
	defer resp.Body.Close()
	ch := llm.NewChunker(m.Name)
	if err := emit(ch.Role()); err != nil {
		return u, err
	}
	err = llm.ReadSSE(resp.Body, func(_, data string) error {
		var r googleResp
		if err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(data), &r); err != nil {
			return fmt.Errorf("bad upstream chunk: %w", err)
		}
		u = r.usage() // usageMetadata is cumulative; keep the latest
		if t := r.text(); t != "" {
			if err := emit(ch.Text(t)); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		if len(r.Candidates) > 0 {
			if f := googleFinish(r.Candidates[0].FinishReason); f != "" {
				return emit(ch.Finish(f))
			}
		}
		return nil
	})
	if err == nil && req.IncludeUsage {
		err = emit(ch.Usage(u))
	}
	return u, err
}

func (g *Google) Embed(context.Context, *llm.Model, []byte) (*llm.Result, error) {
	return nil, fmt.Errorf("%w: embeddings are only supported for openai-compatible models", llm.ErrBadRequest)
}
```

`core/spend/recorder_test.go`:
```go
package spend_test

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"tollgate/core/db/dbtest"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
	"tollgate/core/spend"
)

func TestRecorder_FlushesOnBatchAndOnShutdown(t *testing.T) {
	d := dbtest.New(t)
	s := repo.New(d)
	ctx := context.Background()
	g, _ := s.GetGroupByName(ctx, "default")
	u, _ := s.CreateUser(ctx, "a@x.io", "A", "hunter22hunter22", "admin", g.ID)
	_, k, _ := s.CreateKey(ctx, u.ID, repo.KeyInput{Alias: "k", GroupID: g.ID, BudgetType: "total", BudgetNanos: 1e12})
	m, _ := s.CreateModel(ctx, repo.ModelInput{Name: "m", Provider: "openai", UpstreamModel: "m", Enabled: true, Extra: json.RawMessage(`{}`)})
	rec := func() repo.SpendRecord {
		return repo.SpendRecord{KeyID: k.ID, UserID: u.ID, GroupID: g.ID, ModelID: m.ID, ModelName: "m", Provider: "openai",
			CostNanos: 10, Status: "success", HTTPStatus: 200, CreatedAt: time.Now()}
	}

	r := spend.NewRecorder(s)
	r.FlushEvery = time.Hour // only batch size or shutdown can flush
	r.BatchSize = 2
	runCtx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	go r.Run(runCtx)

	r.Record(rec())
	r.Record(rec()) // hits batch size
	waitFor(t, func() bool { k2, _ := s.GetKey(ctx, k.ID); return k2.SpendNanos == 20 })

	r.Record(rec()) // below batch size; must be flushed by shutdown
	cancel()
	<-r.Done()
	k2, _ := s.GetKey(ctx, k.ID)
	if k2.SpendNanos != 30 {
		t.Fatalf("spend=%d after shutdown drain", k2.SpendNanos)
	}
}

func TestRecorder_BadRecordDoesNotBlockBatch(t *testing.T) {
	s := repo.New(dbtest.New(t))
	ctx := context.Background()
	g, _ := s.GetGroupByName(ctx, "default")
	u, _ := s.CreateUser(ctx, "a@x.io", "A", "hunter22hunter22", "admin", g.ID)
	_, k, _ := s.CreateKey(ctx, u.ID, repo.KeyInput{Alias: "k", GroupID: g.ID, BudgetType: "unlimited"})
	m, _ := s.CreateModel(ctx, repo.ModelInput{Name: "m", Provider: "openai", UpstreamModel: "m", Enabled: true, Extra: json.RawMessage(`{}`)})
	rec := func(modelID string) repo.SpendRecord {
		return repo.SpendRecord{KeyID: k.ID, UserID: u.ID, GroupID: g.ID, ModelID: modelID, ModelName: "m", Provider: "openai",
			CostNanos: 10, Status: "success", HTTPStatus: 200, CreatedAt: time.Now()}
	}

	r := spend.NewRecorder(s)
	r.FlushEvery = time.Hour
	runCtx, cancel := context.WithCancel(ctx)
	go r.Run(runCtx)
	r.Record(rec(m.ID))
	r.Record(rec("00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000")) // model no longer exists
	r.Record(rec(m.ID))
	cancel()
	<-r.Done()
	k2, _ := s.GetKey(ctx, k.ID)
	if k2.SpendNanos != 20 {
		t.Fatalf("spend=%d, want the two valid records (20) despite the bad one", k2.SpendNanos)
	}
}

func waitFor(t *testing.T, cond func() bool) {
	t.Helper()
	deadline := time.Now().Add(5 * time.Second)
	for time.Now().Before(deadline) {
		if cond() {
			return
		}
		time.Sleep(20 * time.Millisecond)
	}
	t.Fatal("condition not met")
}
```

`core/spend/recorder.go`:
```go
// Package spend batches spend records into Postgres off the request path.
package spend

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgconn"

	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

// ponytail: in-process buffer; a crash loses up to FlushEvery of records.
// Move to a durable queue only if that window ever matters.
type Recorder struct {
	FlushEvery time.Duration
	BatchSize  int

	store *repo.Store
	ch    chan repo.SpendRecord
	done  chan struct{}
}

const maxBuffered = 50_000

func NewRecorder(store *repo.Store) *Recorder {
	return &Recorder{FlushEvery: 2 * time.Second, BatchSize: 200, store: store,
		ch: make(chan repo.SpendRecord, 10_000), done: make(chan struct{})}
}

// Record blocks if the buffer is full rather than dropping money data.
func (r *Recorder) Record(rec repo.SpendRecord) { r.ch <- rec }

func (r *Recorder) Done() <-chan struct{} { return r.done }

func (r *Recorder) Run(ctx context.Context) {
	defer close(r.done)
	t := time.NewTicker(r.FlushEvery)
	defer t.Stop()
	var buf []repo.SpendRecord
	flush := func(ctx context.Context) {
		if len(buf) == 0 {
			return
		}
		err := r.store.RecordSpend(ctx, buf)
		var pg *pgconn.PgError
		if errors.As(err, &pg) && strings.HasPrefix(pg.Code, "23") {
			// Integrity error (e.g. a model deleted while its records sat in the buffer):
			// retrying the batch would fail forever and block all billing. Write records
			// one by one and drop only the ones that can't be stored.
			for _, rec := range buf {
				if err := r.store.RecordSpend(ctx, []repo.SpendRecord{rec}); err != nil {
					slog.Error("dropping unrecordable spend record", "key", rec.KeyID, "model", rec.ModelName, "cost_nanos", rec.CostNanos, "err", err)
				}
			}
			buf = nil
			return
		}
		if err != nil {
			slog.Error("spend flush failed; will retry", "records", len(buf), "err", err)
			if len(buf) > maxBuffered {
				slog.Error("spend buffer overflow; dropping oldest records", "dropped", len(buf)-maxBuffered)
				buf = buf[len(buf)-maxBuffered:]
			}
			return
		}
		buf = nil
	}
	for {
		select {
		case rec := <-r.ch:
			buf = append(buf, rec)
			if len(buf) >= r.BatchSize {
				flush(ctx)
			}
		case <-t.C:
			flush(ctx)
		case <-ctx.Done():
			for {
				select {
				case rec := <-r.ch:
					buf = append(buf, rec)
					continue
				default:
				}
				break
			}
			fctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
			flush(fctx)
			cancel()
			return
		}
	}
}
```

`core/worker/worker.go`:
```go
// Package worker runs periodic jobs in-process.
package worker

import (
	"context"
	"log/slog"
	"time"
)

// Every runs fn now and then every d until ctx is cancelled.
func Every(ctx context.Context, name string, d time.Duration, fn func(context.Context) error) {
	go func() {
		run := func() {
			if err := fn(ctx); err != nil && ctx.Err() == nil {
				slog.Error("job failed", "job", name, "err", err)
			}
		}
		run()
		t := time.NewTicker(d)
		defer t.Stop()
		for {
			select {
			case <-ctx.Done():
				return
			case <-t.C:
				run()
			}
		}
	}()
}
```

```go
// failingWriter accepts the first write, then behaves like a disconnected client.
type failingWriter struct {
	*httptest.ResponseRecorder
	writes int
}

func (f *failingWriter) Write(b []byte) (int, error) {
	f.writes++
	if f.writes > 1 {
		return 0, errors.New("client gone")
	}
	return f.ResponseRecorder.Write(b)
}

	}
```

`core/api/server.go`:
```go
// Package api serves the OpenAI-compatible /v1 endpoints.
package api

import (
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"tollgate/core/cache"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
	"tollgate/core/llm"
)

type Server struct {
	Store    *repo.Store
	Cache    *cache.Cache
	Registry *llm.Registry
	Record   func(repo.SpendRecord) // spend.Recorder.Record in production
	Now      func() time.Time
}

const maxBody = 20 << 20

// Upstream calls get their own deadline and survive client disconnects, so
// streams can be drained for exact usage.
const upstreamTimeout = 10 * time.Minute

func (s *Server) Register(mux *http.ServeMux) {
	mux.Handle("POST /v1/chat/completions", s.auth(http.HandlerFunc(s.chat)))
	mux.Handle("POST /v1/embeddings", s.auth(http.HandlerFunc(s.embeddings)))
	mux.Handle("GET /v1/models", s.auth(http.HandlerFunc(s.models)))
	mux.HandleFunc("GET /health", s.health)
}
```

`core/api/errors.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
)

func WriteJSON(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, v any) {
	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	w.WriteHeader(status)
	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(v)
}

// WriteError writes OpenAI's error shape. code is a string, per the OpenAI API.
func WriteError(w http.ResponseWriter, status int, typ, code, msg string) {
	WriteJSON(w, status, map[string]any{"error": map[string]any{"message": msg, "type": typ, "code": code}})
}
```

`core/api/auth.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"strings"

	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

type authKey struct{}

func authFrom(ctx context.Context) *repo.AuthInfo { return ctx.Value(authKey{}).(*repo.AuthInfo) }

func (s *Server) auth(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		token, ok := strings.CutPrefix(r.Header.Get("Authorization"), "Bearer ")
		if !ok || token == "" {
			WriteError(w, 401, "authentication_error", "invalid_api_key", "missing API key")
			return
		}
		info, err := s.Store.AuthByKeyHash(r.Context(), repo.HashToken(token))
		if errors.Is(err, repo.ErrNotFound) {
			WriteError(w, 401, "authentication_error", "invalid_api_key", "invalid API key")
			return
		}
		if err != nil {
			slog.Error("key lookup", "err", err)
			WriteError(w, 500, "api_error", "internal_error", "internal error")
			return
		}
		switch {
		case info.Key.RevokedAt != nil || info.UserDisabled:
			WriteError(w, 403, "permission_error", "key_blocked", "API key is revoked or its owner is disabled")
			return
		case info.Key.ExpiresAt != nil && s.Now().After(*info.Key.ExpiresAt):
			WriteError(w, 403, "permission_error", "key_expired", "API key has expired")
			return
		}
		next.ServeHTTP(w, r.WithContext(context.WithValue(r.Context(), authKey{}, info)))
	})
}
```

`core/api/admit.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"slices"
	"strconv"

	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
	"tollgate/core/llm"
)

type scope struct {
	name, id string
	limits   repo.Limits
}

func scopes(info *repo.AuthInfo) []scope {
	return []scope{{"key", info.Key.ID, info.Key.Limits}, {"group", info.Group.ID, info.Group.Limits}}
}

func modelAllowed(info *repo.AuthInfo, model string) bool {
	return len(info.Key.Models) == 0 || slices.Contains(info.Key.Models, model)
}

// admit enforces budgets, then RPM, then TPM, for both key and group.
// Redis errors fail closed: an unenforceable limit must not become no limit.
func (s *Server) admit(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, info *repo.AuthInfo) bool {
	for _, sc := range scopes(info) {
		if sc.limits.OverBudget() {
			WriteError(w, 429, "insufficient_quota", "budget_exceeded", sc.name+" budget exceeded")
			return false
		}
	}
	now := s.Now()
	retry := strconv.FormatInt(60-now.Unix()%60, 10)
	for _, sc := range scopes(info) {
		if sc.limits.RPMLimit != nil {
			n, err := s.Cache.HitRPM(r.Context(), sc.name, sc.id, now)
			if err != nil {
				slog.Error("rate limiter unavailable", "err", err)
				WriteError(w, 503, "api_error", "rate_limiter_unavailable", "rate limiter unavailable")
				return false
			}
			if n > int64(*sc.limits.RPMLimit) {
				w.Header().Set("Retry-After", retry)
				WriteError(w, 429, "rate_limit_error", "rate_limit_exceeded", fmt.Sprintf("%s RPM limit %d exceeded", sc.name, *sc.limits.RPMLimit))
				return false
			}
		}
		if sc.limits.TPMLimit != nil {
			n, err := s.Cache.TPM(r.Context(), sc.name, sc.id, now)
			if err != nil {
				slog.Error("rate limiter unavailable", "err", err)
				WriteError(w, 503, "api_error", "rate_limiter_unavailable", "rate limiter unavailable")
				return false
			}
			if n >= int64(*sc.limits.TPMLimit) {
				w.Header().Set("Retry-After", retry)
				WriteError(w, 429, "rate_limit_error", "rate_limit_exceeded", fmt.Sprintf("%s TPM limit %d exceeded", sc.name, *sc.limits.TPMLimit))
				return false
			}
		}
	}
	return true
}

// finish records spend and charges TPM. Called once per admitted request that reached upstream.
func (s *Server) finish(info *repo.AuthInfo, rec repo.SpendRecord, u llm.Usage) {
	s.Record(rec)
	if u.Tokens() == 0 {
		if rec.Status == "success" {
			// A provider that stops reporting usage would otherwise bill $0 silently.
			slog.Warn("upstream reported no token usage; request billed $0", "model", rec.ModelName, "provider", rec.Provider)
		}
		return
	}
	now := s.Now()
	for _, sc := range scopes(info) {
		if sc.limits.TPMLimit != nil {
			if err := s.Cache.AddTPM(context.Background(), sc.name, sc.id, u.Tokens(), now); err != nil {
				slog.Error("add TPM", "err", err)
			}
		}
	}
}
```

`core/api/chat.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
	"tollgate/core/llm"
)

func (s *Server) chat(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	info := authFrom(r.Context())
	body, err := io.ReadAll(http.MaxBytesReader(w, r.Body, maxBody))
	if err != nil {
		WriteError(w, 413, "invalid_request_error", "request_too_large", "request body too large")
		return
	}
	req, err := llm.ParseChatRequest(body)
	if err != nil {
		WriteError(w, 400, "invalid_request_error", "invalid_request", err.Error())
		return
	}
	m, p, ok := s.Registry.Get(req.Model)
	if !ok {
		WriteError(w, 404, "invalid_request_error", "model_not_found", fmt.Sprintf("model %q not found", req.Model))
		return
	}
	if !modelAllowed(info, m.Name) {
		WriteError(w, 403, "permission_error", "model_not_allowed", fmt.Sprintf("this key may not use model %q", m.Name))
		return
	}
	if !s.admit(w, r, info) {
		return
	}

	start := s.Now()
	rec := newRecord(info, m, start)
	upCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.WithoutCancel(r.Context()), upstreamTimeout)
	defer cancel()

	var u llm.Usage
	if req.Stream {
		u, err = s.stream(w, r, upCtx, m, p, req)
	} else {
		var res *llm.Result
		if res, err = p.Chat(upCtx, m, req); err == nil {
			u = res.Usage
			w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
			w.Header().Set("X-Tollgate-Cost", llm.FormatUSD(llm.Cost(u, m.Prices)))
			w.WriteHeader(200)
			w.Write(res.Body)
		}
	}
	if errors.Is(err, llm.ErrBadRequest) {
		if !req.Stream || !streamStarted(w) {
			WriteError(w, 400, "invalid_request_error", "unsupported", err.Error())
		}
		return // never reached upstream; nothing to bill
	}
	fill(&rec, u, m, s.Now().Sub(start))
	if err != nil {
		rec.Status, rec.ErrorMessage = "error", err.Error()
		rec.HTTPStatus = 200 // headers already sent for streams
		if !req.Stream || !streamStarted(w) {
			rec.HTTPStatus = writeUpstreamError(w, err)
		}
	}
	s.finish(info, rec, u)
}

// stream proxies SSE. If the client goes away, it stops writing but lets the
// provider drain upstream (upCtx is detached from the client) so usage is exact.
func (s *Server) stream(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, upCtx context.Context, m *llm.Model, p llm.Provider, req *llm.ChatRequest) (llm.Usage, error) {
	sw := &streamWriter{w: w}
	u, err := p.ChatStream(upCtx, m, req, sw.write)
	if sw.gone {
		return u, errors.New("client disconnected mid-stream")
	}
	if err != nil {
		if sw.started {
			b, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]any{"error": map[string]any{"message": err.Error(), "type": "api_error", "code": "upstream_error"}})
			sw.write(b)
		}
		return u, err
	}
	sw.start()
	io.WriteString(w, "data: [DONE]\n\n")
	sw.flush()
	return u, nil
}

type streamWriter struct {
	w             http.ResponseWriter
	started, gone bool
}

func (sw *streamWriter) start() {
	if sw.started {
		return
	}
	sw.started = true
	h := sw.w.Header()
	h.Set("Content-Type", "text/event-stream")
	h.Set("Cache-Control", "no-cache")
	h.Set("X-Accel-Buffering", "no")
	sw.w.WriteHeader(200)
}

func (sw *streamWriter) flush() {
	if f, ok := sw.w.(http.Flusher); ok {
		f.Flush()
	}
}

// write never returns an error: after a client disconnect it silently discards,
// so the provider keeps reading upstream to the end.
func (sw *streamWriter) write(chunk []byte) error {
	if sw.gone {
		return nil
	}
	sw.start()
	if _, err := fmt.Fprintf(sw.w, "data: %s\n\n", chunk); err != nil {
		sw.gone = true
		return nil
	}
	sw.flush()
	return nil
}

// streamStarted reports whether headers went out (so a JSON error can't be written).
func streamStarted(w http.ResponseWriter) bool {
	return w.Header().Get("Content-Type") == "text/event-stream"
}

func writeUpstreamError(w http.ResponseWriter, err error) int {
	var ue *llm.UpstreamError
	if errors.As(err, &ue) {
		status := ue.Status
		if status < 400 || status > 599 {
			status = 502
		}
		WriteError(w, status, "api_error", "upstream_error", ue.Message)
		return status
	}
	WriteError(w, 502, "api_error", "upstream_error", err.Error())
	return 502
}

func newRecord(info *repo.AuthInfo, m *llm.Model, start time.Time) repo.SpendRecord {
	return repo.SpendRecord{KeyID: info.Key.ID, UserID: info.Key.UserID, GroupID: info.Key.GroupID,
		ModelID: m.ID, ModelName: m.Name, Provider: m.Provider, Status: "success", HTTPStatus: 200, CreatedAt: start}
}

func fill(rec *repo.SpendRecord, u llm.Usage, m *llm.Model, latency time.Duration) {
	rec.InputTokens, rec.OutputTokens, rec.CacheReadTokens, rec.CacheWriteTokens = u.Input, u.Output, u.CacheRead, u.CacheWrite
	rec.CostNanos = llm.Cost(u, m.Prices)
	rec.LatencyMS = latency.Milliseconds()
}
```

`core/api/embeddings.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"fmt"
	"io"
	"net/http"

	"tollgate/core/llm"
)

func (s *Server) embeddings(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	info := authFrom(r.Context())
	body, err := io.ReadAll(http.MaxBytesReader(w, r.Body, maxBody))
	if err != nil {
		WriteError(w, 413, "invalid_request_error", "request_too_large", "request body too large")
		return
	}
	var head struct {
		Model string `json:"model"`
	}
	if json.Unmarshal(body, &head) != nil || head.Model == "" {
		WriteError(w, 400, "invalid_request_error", "invalid_request", "model is required")
		return
	}
	m, p, ok := s.Registry.Get(head.Model)
	if !ok {
		WriteError(w, 404, "invalid_request_error", "model_not_found", fmt.Sprintf("model %q not found", head.Model))
		return
	}
	if !modelAllowed(info, m.Name) {
		WriteError(w, 403, "permission_error", "model_not_allowed", fmt.Sprintf("this key may not use model %q", m.Name))
		return
	}
	if !s.admit(w, r, info) {
		return
	}
	start := s.Now()
	rec := newRecord(info, m, start)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.WithoutCancel(r.Context()), upstreamTimeout)
	defer cancel()
	res, err := p.Embed(ctx, m, body)
	if errors.Is(err, llm.ErrBadRequest) {
		WriteError(w, 400, "invalid_request_error", "unsupported", err.Error())
		return
	}
	var u llm.Usage
	if err != nil {
		rec.Status, rec.ErrorMessage = "error", err.Error()
		rec.HTTPStatus = writeUpstreamError(w, err)
	} else {
		u = res.Usage
		w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
		w.Header().Set("X-Tollgate-Cost", llm.FormatUSD(llm.Cost(u, m.Prices)))
		w.WriteHeader(200)
		w.Write(res.Body)
	}
	fill(&rec, u, m, s.Now().Sub(start))
	s.finish(info, rec, u)
}
```

`core/api/models.go`:
```go
package api

import "net/http"

func (s *Server) models(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	info := authFrom(r.Context())
	data := []map[string]any{}
	for _, m := range s.Registry.List() {
		if modelAllowed(info, m.Name) {
			data = append(data, map[string]any{"id": m.Name, "object": "model", "created": m.Created, "owned_by": m.Provider})
		}
	}
	WriteJSON(w, 200, map[string]any{"object": "list", "data": data})
}
```

`core/api/health.go`:
```go
package api

import (
	"context"
	"net/http"
	"time"
)

func (s *Server) health(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(r.Context(), 2*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	out := map[string]string{"postgres": "ok", "redis": "ok"}
	status := 200
	if err := s.Store.Ping(ctx); err != nil {
		out["postgres"], status = "down", 503
	}
	if err := s.Cache.Ping(ctx); err != nil {
		out["redis"], status = "down", 503
	}
	WriteJSON(w, status, out)
}
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: `tollgate` (serve) and `tollgate admin create --email E --name N` (password from `TOLLGATE_ADMIN_PASSWORD` or an interactive prompt). `serve` exposes `mux *http.ServeMux` and `reload func(context.Context) error` that Tasks 16–17 hook into.

`cmd/tollgate/main.go`:
```go
package main

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"flag"
	"fmt"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"strings"
	"syscall"
	"time"

	"golang.org/x/term"

	"tollgate/core/api"
	"tollgate/core/cache"
	"tollgate/core/config"
	"tollgate/core/db"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
	"tollgate/core/llm"
	"tollgate/core/llm/providers"
	"tollgate/core/spend"
	"tollgate/core/vault"
	"tollgate/core/worker"
)

func main() {
	slog.SetDefault(slog.New(slog.NewJSONHandler(os.Stdout, nil)))
	var err error
	if len(os.Args) > 2 && os.Args[1] == "admin" && os.Args[2] == "create" {
		err = adminCreate(os.Args[3:])
	} else if len(os.Args) > 1 && os.Args[1] != "serve" {
		err = fmt.Errorf("usage: tollgate [serve] | tollgate admin create --email E --name N")
	} else {
		err = serve()
	}
	if err != nil {
		slog.Error("fatal", "err", err)
		os.Exit(1)
	}
}

func serve() error {
	ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt, syscall.SIGTERM)
	defer stop()

	cfg, err := config.Load()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	d, err := db.Open(ctx, cfg.DatabaseURL)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer d.Close()
	if err := d.Migrate(ctx); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	c, err := cache.New(cfg.RedisURL)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer c.Close()
	if err := c.Ping(ctx); err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("redis: %w", err)
	}
	v, err := vault.New(cfg.EncryptionKey)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	store := repo.New(d)

	httpClient := &http.Client{Timeout: 10 * time.Minute}
	reg := llm.NewRegistry(map[string]llm.Provider{
		"openai":    &providers.OpenAI{HTTP: httpClient},
		"anthropic": &providers.Anthropic{HTTP: httpClient},
		"google":    providers.NewGoogle(httpClient),
	})
	reload := func(ctx context.Context) error {
		ms, err := llm.LoadModels(ctx, store, v)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		reg.Replace(ms)
		slog.Info("models loaded", "count", len(ms))
		return nil
	}
	if err := reload(ctx); err != nil {
		return err
	}

	rec := spend.NewRecorder(store)
	recCtx, recCancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	go rec.Run(recCtx)

	worker.Every(ctx, "budget_reset", time.Minute, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		_, err := store.ResetBudgets(ctx)
		return err
	})
	worker.Every(ctx, "spend_retention", 24*time.Hour, func(ctx context.Context) error {
		n, err := store.DeleteSpendBefore(ctx, time.Now().AddDate(0, 0, -cfg.SpendRetentionDays))
		if n > 0 {
			slog.Info("spend logs pruned", "rows", n)
		}
		return err
	})

	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	(&api.Server{Store: store, Cache: c, Registry: reg, Record: rec.Record, Now: time.Now}).Register(mux)
	// Task 16 registers the console API here; Task 17 mounts the SPA.

	// No WriteTimeout: streams can run for minutes.
	srv := &http.Server{Addr: cfg.Addr, Handler: mux, ReadHeaderTimeout: 10 * time.Second, IdleTimeout: 120 * time.Second}
	errc := make(chan error, 1)
	go func() {
		slog.Info("listening", "addr", cfg.Addr)
		errc <- srv.ListenAndServe()
	}()
	select {
	case <-ctx.Done():
	case err := <-errc:
		if !errors.Is(err, http.ErrServerClosed) {
			return err
		}
	}
	slog.Info("shutting down")
	shutCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	if err := srv.Shutdown(shutCtx); err != nil {
		slog.Error("http shutdown", "err", err)
	}
	recCancel() // after HTTP drains, so no handler records into a stopped recorder
	<-rec.Done()
	return nil
}

func adminCreate(args []string) error {
	fs := flag.NewFlagSet("admin create", flag.ExitOnError)
	email := fs.String("email", "", "admin email")
	name := fs.String("name", "", "display name")
	fs.Parse(args)
	if *email == "" || *name == "" {
		return errors.New("--email and --name are required")
	}
	pw := os.Getenv("TOLLGATE_ADMIN_PASSWORD")
	if pw == "" {
		fmt.Fprint(os.Stderr, "Password: ")
		b1, err := term.ReadPassword(int(os.Stdin.Fd()))
		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		fmt.Fprint(os.Stderr, "Confirm:  ")
		b2, _ := term.ReadPassword(int(os.Stdin.Fd()))
		fmt.Fprintln(os.Stderr)
		if string(b1) != string(b2) {
			return errors.New("passwords do not match")
		}
		pw = string(b1)
	}
	if len(pw) < 12 {
		return errors.New("password must be at least 12 characters")
	}
	ctx := context.Background()
	url := os.Getenv("DATABASE_URL")
	if url == "" {
		return errors.New("DATABASE_URL is required")
	}
	d, err := db.Open(ctx, url)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	defer d.Close()
	if err := d.Migrate(ctx); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	s := repo.New(d)
	g, err := s.GetGroupByName(ctx, "default")
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	u, err := s.CreateUser(ctx, *email, strings.TrimSpace(*name), pw, "admin", g.ID)
	if errors.Is(err, repo.ErrConflict) {
		return fmt.Errorf("user %s already exists", *email)
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	s.InsertAudit(ctx, repo.AuditEntry{ActorEmail: "cli", Action: "user.create", Resource: "users", ResourceID: u.ID})
	fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "created admin %s (%s)\n", u.Email, u.ID)
	return nil
}
```

- Produces:

  - `type auth.Sessions struct{ Cache *cache.Cache; Store *repo.Store; Insecure bool; TTL time.Duration }`; `CookieName = "tollgate_session"`; methods `Start(ctx, w, userID string) error`, `End(w, r)`, `Load(next http.Handler) http.Handler` (attaches the user if the cookie is valid and the user isn't disabled), `Require(p rbac.Permission, h http.HandlerFunc) http.Handler` (401 without session, 403 without permission; `p == ""` means any signed-in user)
  - `auth.UserFrom(ctx) *repo.User` (nil when anonymous)
  - `auth.CSRF(baseURL string, next http.Handler) http.Handler`
  - `auth.ClientIP(r *http.Request, trusted []netip.Prefix) string`

```go
package rbac

import "testing"

func TestRoles(t *testing.T) {
	for _, p := range Permissions("admin") {
		if !Has("admin", p) {
			t.Fatalf("admin missing %s", p)
		}
	}
	if len(Permissions("admin")) != 18 {
		t.Fatalf("admin has %d permissions", len(Permissions("admin")))
	}
	if !Has("member", KeysWriteOwn) || Has("member", KeysWriteAll) || Has("member", UsersRead) {
		t.Fatal("member permissions wrong")
	}
	if Has("viewer", KeysWriteOwn) || !Has("viewer", KeysReadOwn) || !Has("viewer", SpendReadOwn) {
		t.Fatal("viewer permissions wrong")
	}
	if Has("nobody", ModelsRead) {
		t.Fatal("unknown role has permissions")
	}
}
```

`console/auth/auth_test.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"net/netip"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/alicebob/miniredis/v2"
	"github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"

	"tollgate/console/rbac"
	"tollgate/core/cache"
	"tollgate/core/db/dbtest"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

func TestClientIP(t *testing.T) {
	trusted := []netip.Prefix{netip.MustParsePrefix("10.0.0.0/8")}
	r := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/", nil)
	r.RemoteAddr = "10.1.1.1:5555"
	r.Header.Set("X-Forwarded-For", "6.6.6.6, 1.2.3.4, 10.2.2.2")
	if ip := ClientIP(r, trusted); ip != "1.2.3.4" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s (must skip trusted hops from the right, not trust the leftmost)", ip)
	}
	r.RemoteAddr = "8.8.8.8:5555" // untrusted peer: ignore XFF entirely
	if ip := ClientIP(r, trusted); ip != "8.8.8.8" {
		t.Fatalf("got %s", ip)
	}
}

func TestCSRF(t *testing.T) {
	h := CSRF("https://gw.example.com", http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) { w.WriteHeader(204) }))
	cases := []struct {
		method, path, ct, origin string
		want                     int
	}{
		{"POST", "/console/api/v1/keys", "application/json", "https://gw.example.com", 204},
		{"POST", "/console/api/v1/keys", "application/json", "", 204},
		{"POST", "/console/api/v1/keys", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded", "", 403},
		{"DELETE", "/console/api/v1/keys/1", "application/json", "https://evil.example", 403},
		{"POST", "/auth/login", "text/plain", "", 403},
		{"GET", "/console/api/v1/keys", "", "https://evil.example", 204},
		{"POST", "/v1/chat/completions", "text/plain", "https://evil.example", 204}, // API keys, not cookies
	}
	for _, c := range cases {
		r := httptest.NewRequest(c.method, c.path, strings.NewReader("{}"))
		if c.ct != "" {
			r.Header.Set("Content-Type", c.ct)
		}
		if c.origin != "" {
			r.Header.Set("Origin", c.origin)
		}
		w := httptest.NewRecorder()
		h.ServeHTTP(w, r)
		if w.Code != c.want {
			t.Errorf("%s %s ct=%q origin=%q: got %d want %d", c.method, c.path, c.ct, c.origin, w.Code, c.want)
		}
	}
}

func TestSessions_StartLoadRequireEnd(t *testing.T) {
	s := repo.New(dbtest.New(t))
	ctx := context.Background()
	g, _ := s.GetGroupByName(ctx, "default")
	u, _ := s.CreateUser(ctx, "m@x.io", "M", "hunter22hunter22", "member", g.ID)
	mr := miniredis.RunT(t)
	sess := &Sessions{Cache: cache.FromClient(redis.NewClient(&redis.Options{Addr: mr.Addr()})), Store: s, TTL: time.Hour}

	w := httptest.NewRecorder()
	if err := sess.Start(ctx, w, u.ID); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	cookie := w.Result().Cookies()[0]
	if cookie.Name != CookieName || !cookie.HttpOnly || !cookie.Secure || cookie.SameSite != http.SameSiteLaxMode {
		t.Fatalf("cookie flags: %+v", cookie)
	}

	ok := http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) { w.WriteHeader(200) })
	call := func(h http.Handler, withCookie bool) int {
		r := httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/", nil)
		if withCookie {
			r.AddCookie(cookie)
		}
		rw := httptest.NewRecorder()
		sess.Load(h).ServeHTTP(rw, r)
		return rw.Code
	}
	if c := call(sess.Require(rbac.KeysReadOwn, ok), true); c != 200 {
		t.Fatalf("member reading own keys: %d", c)
	}
	if c := call(sess.Require(rbac.UsersRead, ok), true); c != 403 {
		t.Fatalf("member reading users: %d", c)
	}
	if c := call(sess.Require(rbac.KeysReadOwn, ok), false); c != 401 {
		t.Fatalf("anonymous: %d", c)
	}

	disabled := true
	s.UpdateUser(ctx, u.ID, repo.UserUpdate{Disabled: &disabled})
	if c := call(sess.Require("", ok), true); c != 401 {
		t.Fatalf("disabled user kept session: %d", c)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `rbac.go`**

`console/rbac/rbac.go`:
```go
// Package rbac defines console permissions. Roles live in code, not the DB.
package rbac

import "slices"

type Permission string

const (
	ModelsRead    Permission = "models:read"
	ModelsWrite   Permission = "models:write"
	KeysReadOwn   Permission = "keys:read_own"
	KeysWriteOwn  Permission = "keys:write_own"
	KeysReadAll   Permission = "keys:read_all"
	KeysWriteAll  Permission = "keys:write_all"
	UsersRead     Permission = "users:read"
	UsersWrite    Permission = "users:write"
	GroupsReadOwn Permission = "groups:read_own"
	GroupsReadAll Permission = "groups:read_all"
	GroupsWrite   Permission = "groups:write"
	InvitesRead   Permission = "invites:read"
	InvitesWrite  Permission = "invites:write"
	SpendReadOwn  Permission = "spend:read_own"
	SpendReadAll  Permission = "spend:read_all"
	AuditRead     Permission = "audit:read"
	SettingsRead  Permission = "settings:read"
	SettingsWrite Permission = "settings:write"
)

var viewer = []Permission{ModelsRead, KeysReadOwn, GroupsReadOwn, SpendReadOwn}

var roles = map[string][]Permission{
	"viewer": viewer,
	"member": append(slices.Clone(viewer), KeysWriteOwn),
	"admin": {ModelsRead, ModelsWrite, KeysReadOwn, KeysWriteOwn, KeysReadAll, KeysWriteAll, UsersRead, UsersWrite,
		GroupsReadOwn, GroupsReadAll, GroupsWrite, InvitesRead, InvitesWrite, SpendReadOwn, SpendReadAll, AuditRead, SettingsRead, SettingsWrite},
}

func Permissions(role string) []Permission { return roles[role] }

func Has(role string, p Permission) bool { return slices.Contains(roles[role], p) }

func ValidRole(role string) bool { _, ok := roles[role]; return ok }
```

`console/auth/session.go`:
```go
// Package auth handles console sessions and request hygiene.
package auth

import (
	"context"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"tollgate/console/rbac"
	"tollgate/core/api"
	"tollgate/core/cache"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

const CookieName = "tollgate_session"

type Sessions struct {
	Cache    *cache.Cache
	Store    *repo.Store
	Insecure bool // COOKIE_INSECURE: drop the Secure flag for plain-http local dev
	TTL      time.Duration
}

type userKey struct{}

func UserFrom(ctx context.Context) *repo.User {
	u, _ := ctx.Value(userKey{}).(*repo.User)
	return u
}

func (s *Sessions) Start(ctx context.Context, w http.ResponseWriter, userID string) error {
	token := repo.RandomToken(32)
	if err := s.Cache.SetSession(ctx, repo.HashToken(token), userID, s.TTL); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	http.SetCookie(w, &http.Cookie{Name: CookieName, Value: token, Path: "/", HttpOnly: true, Secure: !s.Insecure,
		SameSite: http.SameSiteLaxMode, MaxAge: int(s.TTL.Seconds())})
	return nil
}

func (s *Sessions) End(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if c, err := r.Cookie(CookieName); err == nil {
		s.Cache.DelSession(r.Context(), repo.HashToken(c.Value))
	}
	http.SetCookie(w, &http.Cookie{Name: CookieName, Value: "", Path: "/", HttpOnly: true, Secure: !s.Insecure, MaxAge: -1})
}

// Load attaches the session user to the context. The user row is re-read on
// every request so role changes and disables apply immediately.
func (s *Sessions) Load(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		c, err := r.Cookie(CookieName)
		if err != nil {
			next.ServeHTTP(w, r)
			return
		}
		id, err := s.Cache.GetSession(r.Context(), repo.HashToken(c.Value))
		if err != nil {
			slog.Error("session lookup", "err", err)
		}
		if id != "" {
			if u, err := s.Store.GetUser(r.Context(), id); err == nil && !u.Disabled {
				r = r.WithContext(context.WithValue(r.Context(), userKey{}, u))
			}
		}
		next.ServeHTTP(w, r)
	})
}

func (s *Sessions) Require(p rbac.Permission, h http.HandlerFunc) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		u := UserFrom(r.Context())
		if u == nil {
			api.WriteError(w, 401, "authentication_error", "not_signed_in", "sign in required")
			return
		}
		if p != "" && !rbac.Has(u.Role, p) {
			api.WriteError(w, 403, "permission_error", "forbidden", "you don't have permission to do that")
			return
		}
		h(w, r)
	})
}
```

`console/auth/csrf.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"mime"
	"net/http"
	"strings"

	"tollgate/core/api"
)

// CSRF guards cookie-authenticated routes: state-changing requests must be JSON
// (a cross-site HTML form can't send that) and, if the browser sends Origin, it
// must be ours. /v1 uses API keys, not cookies, so it's exempt.
func CSRF(baseURL string, next http.Handler) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		cookieRoute := strings.HasPrefix(r.URL.Path, "/auth/") || strings.HasPrefix(r.URL.Path, "/console/")
		safe := r.Method == http.MethodGet || r.Method == http.MethodHead || r.Method == http.MethodOptions
		if cookieRoute && !safe {
			mt, _, _ := mime.ParseMediaType(r.Header.Get("Content-Type"))
			origin := r.Header.Get("Origin")
			if mt != "application/json" || (origin != "" && origin != baseURL) {
				api.WriteError(w, 403, "permission_error", "csrf", "cross-site request rejected")
				return
			}
		}
		next.ServeHTTP(w, r)
	})
}
```

`console/auth/clientip.go`:
```go
package auth

import (
	"net"
	"net/http"
	"net/netip"
	"strings"
)

func inAny(ip netip.Addr, nets []netip.Prefix) bool {
	for _, n := range nets {
		if n.Contains(ip) {
			return true
		}
	}
	return false
}

// ClientIP honors X-Forwarded-For only when the direct peer is a trusted proxy,
// and then takes the rightmost address that isn't itself a trusted proxy.
func ClientIP(r *http.Request, trusted []netip.Prefix) string {
	host, _, err := net.SplitHostPort(r.RemoteAddr)
	if err != nil {
		host = r.RemoteAddr
	}
	peer, err := netip.ParseAddr(host)
	if err != nil || !inAny(peer.Unmap(), trusted) {
		return host
	}
	hops := strings.Split(r.Header.Get("X-Forwarded-For"), ",")
	for i := len(hops) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
		ip, err := netip.ParseAddr(strings.TrimSpace(hops[i]))
		if err != nil {
			break
		}
		if !inAny(ip.Unmap(), trusted) {
			return ip.String()
		}
	}
	return host
}
```

- Produces: `type consoleapi.Server struct{ Store *repo.Store; Cache *cache.Cache; Sessions *auth.Sessions; Vault *vault.Vault; Reload func(context.Context) error; BaseURL string; TrustedProxies []netip.Prefix }` (package name `consoleapi`, directory `console/api`); `(*Server).Register(mux *http.ServeMux)`. Routes added in this task:

| Route | Permission |
|-------|------------|
| `POST /auth/login` | public |
| `POST /auth/logout` | signed in |
| `GET /auth/me` | signed in |
| `GET /auth/invite/{token}`, `POST /auth/invite/{token}` | public |
| `GET /console/api/v1/invites`, `POST …/invites`, `DELETE …/invites/{id}` | `invites:read` / `invites:write` |
| `GET …/users`, `PUT …/users/{id}`, `DELETE …/users/{id}` (disable) | `users:read` / `users:write` |
| `GET …/groups` | `groups:read_own` (all groups when also `groups:read_all`) |
| `POST …/groups`, `PUT …/groups/{id}` | `groups:write` |
| `GET …/keys`, `POST …/keys`, `PUT …/keys/{id}`, `DELETE …/keys/{id}` | `keys:read_own` / `keys:write_own` (scoped to own keys unless `keys:*_all`) |

`console/api/testenv_test.go`:
```go
package consoleapi

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/alicebob/miniredis/v2"
	"github.com/redis/go-redis/v9"

	"tollgate/console/auth"
	"tollgate/core/cache"
	"tollgate/core/db/dbtest"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
	"tollgate/core/vault"
)

const base = "https://gw.example.com"

type tenv struct {
	t        *testing.T
	h        http.Handler
	store    *repo.Store
	group    *repo.Group
	admin    *repo.User
	member   *repo.User
	viewer   *repo.User
	reloads  int
	cookies  map[string]*http.Cookie
}

func newTenv(t *testing.T) *tenv {
	s := repo.New(dbtest.New(t))
	ctx := context.Background()
	g, _ := s.GetGroupByName(ctx, "default")
	e := &tenv{t: t, store: s, group: g, cookies: map[string]*http.Cookie{}}
	e.admin, _ = s.CreateUser(ctx, "admin@x.io", "Admin", "hunter22hunter22", "admin", g.ID)
	e.member, _ = s.CreateUser(ctx, "member@x.io", "Member", "hunter22hunter22", "member", g.ID)
	e.viewer, _ = s.CreateUser(ctx, "viewer@x.io", "Viewer", "hunter22hunter22", "viewer", g.ID)
	mr := miniredis.RunT(t)
	c := cache.FromClient(redis.NewClient(&redis.Options{Addr: mr.Addr()}))
	v, _ := vault.New(bytes.Repeat([]byte{3}, 32))
	srv := &Server{Store: s, Cache: c, Vault: v, BaseURL: base,
		Sessions: &auth.Sessions{Cache: c, Store: s, TTL: time.Hour},
		Reload:   func(context.Context) error { e.reloads++; return nil }}
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	srv.Register(mux)
	e.h = auth.CSRF(base, mux)
	return e
}

// as performs a request as the given email ("" = anonymous), logging in on first use.
func (e *tenv) as(email, method, path string, body any) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
	e.t.Helper()
	if email != "" && e.cookies[email] == nil {
		w := e.raw("", method, "/auth/login", map[string]string{"email": email, "password": "hunter22hunter22"})
		if w.Code != 200 {
			e.t.Fatalf("login %s: %d %s", email, w.Code, w.Body)
		}
		e.cookies[email] = w.Result().Cookies()[0]
	}
	return e.raw(email, method, path, body)
}

func (e *tenv) raw(email, method, path string, body any) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
	var rdr *bytes.Reader
	if body != nil {
		b, _ := json.Marshal(body)
		rdr = bytes.NewReader(b)
	} else {
		rdr = bytes.NewReader(nil)
	}
	r := httptest.NewRequest(method, path, rdr)
	r.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
	if c := e.cookies[email]; email != "" && c != nil {
		r.AddCookie(c)
	}
	w := httptest.NewRecorder()
	e.h.ServeHTTP(w, r)
	return w
}

func decodeInto[T any](t *testing.T, w *httptest.ResponseRecorder) T {
	t.Helper()
	var v T
	if err := json.Unmarshal(w.Body.Bytes(), &v); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("decode %s: %v", w.Body, err)
	}
	return v
}
```

```go
package consoleapi

import (

func TestLogin_FailsGenericallyAndThrottles(t *testing.T) {
	e := newTenv(t)
	for i := 0; i < 10; i++ {
		w := e.raw("", "POST", "/auth/login", map[string]string{"email": "member@x.io", "password": "wrong"})
		if w.Code != 401 || !strings.Contains(w.Body.String(), "invalid email or password") {
			t.Fatalf("attempt %d: %d %s", i, w.Code, w.Body)
		}
	}
	w := e.raw("", "POST", "/auth/login", map[string]string{"email": "member@x.io", "password": "hunter22hunter22"})
	if w.Code != 429 {
		t.Fatalf("locked account accepted correct password: %d", w.Code)
	}
	if w := e.raw("", "POST", "/auth/login", map[string]string{"email": "ghost@x.io", "password": "x"}); w.Code != 429 && w.Code != 401 {
		t.Fatalf("unknown user: %d", w.Code)
	}
}

func TestMe_ReturnsPermissions(t *testing.T) {
	e := newTenv(t)
	me := decodeInto[struct {
		User        repo.User
		Permissions []string
	}](t, e.as("viewer@x.io", "GET", "/auth/me", nil))
	if me.User.Email != "viewer@x.io" || len(me.Permissions) != 4 {
		t.Fatalf("%+v", me)
	}
}

func TestInvite_EndToEnd(t *testing.T) {
	e := newTenv(t)
	team, _ := e.store.CreateGroup(context.Background(), repo.GroupInput{Name: "team", BudgetType: "unlimited"})
	w := e.as("admin@x.io", "POST", "/console/api/v1/invites", map[string]any{"role": "member", "group_id": team.ID, "ttl_hours": 24})
	if w.Code != 201 {
		t.Fatalf("create: %d %s", w.Code, w.Body)
	}
	out := decodeInto[struct{ URL string }](t, w)
	token, ok := strings.CutPrefix(out.URL, base+"/invite/")
	if !ok {
		t.Fatalf("url %q", out.URL)
	}
	info := decodeInto[struct{ Role, GroupName, Status string }](t, e.raw("", "GET", "/auth/invite/"+token, nil))
	if info.Role != "member" || info.Status != "active" {
		t.Fatalf("info %+v", info)
	}
	w = e.raw("", "POST", "/auth/invite/"+token, map[string]string{"email": "new@x.io", "name": "New", "password": "short"})
	if w.Code != 400 {
		t.Fatalf("short password accepted: %d", w.Code)
	}
	w = e.raw("", "POST", "/auth/invite/"+token, map[string]string{"email": "new@x.io", "name": "New", "password": "a-long-password"})
	if w.Code != 201 || len(w.Result().Cookies()) == 0 {
		t.Fatalf("accept: %d %s", w.Code, w.Body)
	}
	if w := e.raw("", "POST", "/auth/invite/"+token, map[string]string{"email": "n2@x.io", "name": "N", "password": "a-long-password"}); w.Code != 410 {
		t.Fatalf("reuse: %d", w.Code)
	}
}

func TestUsers_AdminCannotLockThemselvesOut(t *testing.T) {

	if w := e.as("admin@x.io", "PUT", "/console/api/v1/users/"+e.admin.ID, map[string]any{"role": "member"}); w.Code != 400 {

func TestGroups_MemberSeesOnlyOwn(t *testing.T) {

	gs := decodeInto[[]repo.Group](t, e.as("member@x.io", "GET", "/console/api/v1/groups", nil))

	if len(all) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("admin sees %d", len(all))
	}
}
```

```go
package consoleapi

func TestKeys_CreateReturnsRawOnceAndAuditsWithoutSecret(t *testing.T) {
```

`console/api/server.go`:
```go
// Package consoleapi serves /auth/* and /console/api/v1/* for the web console.
package consoleapi

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"net/netip"

	"tollgate/console/auth"
	"tollgate/console/rbac"
	"tollgate/core/api"
	"tollgate/core/cache"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
	"tollgate/core/vault"
)

type Server struct {
	Store          *repo.Store
	Cache          *cache.Cache
	Sessions       *auth.Sessions
	Vault          *vault.Vault
	Reload         func(context.Context) error // rebuild the model registry after model/credential writes
	BaseURL        string
	TrustedProxies []netip.Prefix
}

const p = "/console/api/v1"

func (s *Server) Register(mux *http.ServeMux) {
	req := s.Sessions.Require
	routes := map[string]http.Handler{
		"POST /auth/login":           http.HandlerFunc(s.login),
		"POST /auth/logout":          req("", s.logout),
		"GET /auth/me":               req("", s.me),
		"GET /auth/invite/{token}":   http.HandlerFunc(s.inviteInfo),
		"POST /auth/invite/{token}":  http.HandlerFunc(s.inviteAccept),
		"GET " + p + "/invites":         req(rbac.InvitesRead, s.listInvites),
		"POST " + p + "/invites":        req(rbac.InvitesWrite, s.createInvite),
		"DELETE " + p + "/invites/{id}": req(rbac.InvitesWrite, s.revokeInvite),
		"GET " + p + "/users":           req(rbac.UsersRead, s.listUsers),
		"PUT " + p + "/users/{id}":      req(rbac.UsersWrite, s.updateUser),
		"DELETE " + p + "/users/{id}":   req(rbac.UsersWrite, s.disableUser),
		"GET " + p + "/groups":          req(rbac.GroupsReadOwn, s.listGroups),
		"POST " + p + "/groups":         req(rbac.GroupsWrite, s.createGroup),
		"PUT " + p + "/groups/{id}":     req(rbac.GroupsWrite, s.updateGroup),
		"GET " + p + "/keys":            req(rbac.KeysReadOwn, s.listKeys),
		"POST " + p + "/keys":           req(rbac.KeysWriteOwn, s.createKey),
		"PUT " + p + "/keys/{id}":       req(rbac.KeysWriteOwn, s.updateKey),
		"DELETE " + p + "/keys/{id}":    req(rbac.KeysWriteOwn, s.revokeKey),
	}
	for pattern, h := range s.extraRoutes() { // Task 16
		routes[pattern] = h
	}
	for pattern, h := range routes {
		mux.Handle(pattern, s.Sessions.Load(h))
	}
}

// decode reads a JSON body (max 1 MiB) into v, writing a 400 on failure.
func decode(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, v any) bool {
	if err := json.NewDecoder(http.MaxBytesReader(w, r.Body, 1<<20)).Decode(v); err != nil {
		api.WriteError(w, 400, "invalid_request_error", "invalid_json", "invalid JSON body")
		return false
	}
	return true
}

func badRequest(w http.ResponseWriter, msg string) {
	api.WriteError(w, 400, "invalid_request_error", "invalid_request", msg)
}

func notFound(w http.ResponseWriter) {
	api.WriteError(w, 404, "invalid_request_error", "not_found", "not found")
}

// storeErr maps repo errors to HTTP; anything unexpected is logged and hidden.
func storeErr(w http.ResponseWriter, err error) {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, repo.ErrNotFound):
		notFound(w)
	case errors.Is(err, repo.ErrConflict):
		api.WriteError(w, 409, "invalid_request_error", "conflict", "already exists or still in use")
	default:
		slog.Error("console store error", "err", err)
		api.WriteError(w, 500, "api_error", "internal_error", "internal error")
	}
}

func validBudget(t string) bool {
	return t == "unlimited" || t == "daily" || t == "monthly" || t == "total"
}
```

`console/api/audit.go`:
```go
package consoleapi

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"

	"tollgate/console/auth"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

// audit records a console mutation. before/after are marshalled with their
// json tags, which already exclude password hashes, key hashes, and ciphertext.
func (s *Server) audit(r *http.Request, action, resource, id string, before, after any) {
	details, _ := json.Marshal(map[string]any{"before": before, "after": after})
	e := repo.AuditEntry{Action: action, Resource: resource, ResourceID: id, IP: auth.ClientIP(r, s.TrustedProxies), Details: details}
	if u := auth.UserFrom(r.Context()); u != nil {
		e.ActorID, e.ActorEmail = &u.ID, u.Email
	}
	if err := s.Store.InsertAudit(r.Context(), e); err != nil {
		slog.Error("audit write failed", "action", action, "err", err)
	}
}
```
