`console/api/login.go`:
```go
package consoleapi

import (
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"

	"golang.org/x/crypto/bcrypt"

	"tollgate/console/auth"
	"tollgate/console/rbac"
	"tollgate/core/api"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

// dummyHash makes unknown-email logins as slow as wrong-password ones, so
// response time doesn't reveal which emails have accounts.
var dummyHash, _ = bcrypt.GenerateFromPassword([]byte("tollgate-dummy-password"), 12)

func (s *Server) login(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var in struct{ Email, Password string }
	if !decode(w, r, &in) {
		return
	}
	ip := auth.ClientIP(r, s.TrustedProxies)
	if blocked, err := s.Cache.LoginBlocked(r.Context(), ip, in.Email); err != nil || blocked {
		if err != nil {
			slog.Error("login throttle", "err", err)
		}
		api.WriteError(w, 429, "rate_limit_error", "too_many_attempts", "too many failed attempts; try again in 15 minutes")
		return
	}
	u, err := s.Store.GetUserByEmail(r.Context(), in.Email)
	if err != nil {
		bcrypt.CompareHashAndPassword(dummyHash, []byte(in.Password))
	}
	if err != nil || u.Disabled || !repo.CheckPassword(u, in.Password) {
		s.Cache.RecordLoginFailure(r.Context(), ip, in.Email)
		api.WriteError(w, 401, "authentication_error", "invalid_credentials", "invalid email or password")
		return
	}
	s.Cache.ClearLoginFailures(r.Context(), in.Email)
	if err := s.Sessions.Start(r.Context(), w, u.ID); err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.writeMe(w, r, u)
}

func (s *Server) logout(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	s.Sessions.End(w, r)
	w.WriteHeader(204)
}

func (s *Server) me(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) { s.writeMe(w, r, auth.UserFrom(r.Context())) }

func (s *Server) writeMe(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, u *repo.User) {
	st, err := s.Store.GetSettings(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, map[string]any{"user": u, "permissions": rbac.Permissions(u.Role), "server": st})
}
```

`console/api/invites.go`:
```go
package consoleapi

import (
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"tollgate/console/auth"
	"tollgate/console/rbac"
	"tollgate/core/api"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

type inviteView struct {
	repo.Invite
	Status string `json:"status"`
}

func (s *Server) listInvites(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	invs, err := s.Store.ListInvites(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	out := make([]inviteView, len(invs))
	for i := range invs {
		out[i] = inviteView{invs[i], invs[i].Status()}
	}
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, out)
}

func (s *Server) createInvite(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var in struct {
		Role     string `json:"role"`
		GroupID  string `json:"group_id"`
		Email    string `json:"email"`
		TTLHours int    `json:"ttl_hours"`
	}
	if !decode(w, r, &in) {
		return
	}
	if !rbac.ValidRole(in.Role) {
		badRequest(w, "role must be admin, member, or viewer")
		return
	}
	if _, err := s.Store.GetGroup(r.Context(), in.GroupID); err != nil {
		badRequest(w, "unknown group")
		return
	}
	if in.TTLHours <= 0 || in.TTLHours > 24*30 {
		in.TTLHours = 24 * 7
	}
	me := auth.UserFrom(r.Context())
	token, inv, err := s.Store.CreateInvite(r.Context(), me.ID, in.Role, in.GroupID, in.Email, time.Duration(in.TTLHours)*time.Hour)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "invite.create", "invites", inv.ID, nil, inv)
	api.WriteJSON(w, 201, map[string]any{"invite": inviteView{*inv, inv.Status()}, "url": s.BaseURL + "/invite/" + token})
}

func (s *Server) revokeInvite(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id := r.PathValue("id")
	if err := s.Store.RevokeInvite(r.Context(), id); err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "invite.revoke", "invites", id, nil, nil)
	w.WriteHeader(204)
}

func (s *Server) inviteInfo(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	inv, err := s.Store.GetInviteByToken(r.Context(), r.PathValue("token"))
	if err != nil {
		notFound(w)
		return
	}
	g, err := s.Store.GetGroup(r.Context(), inv.GroupID)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, map[string]any{"role": inv.Role, "group_name": g.Name, "email": inv.Email, "status": inv.Status()})
}

func (s *Server) inviteAccept(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var in struct{ Email, Name, Password string }
	if !decode(w, r, &in) {
		return
	}
	if len(in.Password) < 12 {
		badRequest(w, "password must be at least 12 characters")
		return
	}
	if in.Email == "" || in.Name == "" {
		badRequest(w, "email and name are required")
		return
	}
	u, err := s.Store.AcceptInvite(r.Context(), r.PathValue("token"), in.Email, in.Name, in.Password)
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, repo.ErrInviteUnusable):
		api.WriteError(w, 410, "invalid_request_error", "invite_unusable", err.Error())
		return
	case errors.Is(err, repo.ErrConflict):
		api.WriteError(w, 409, "invalid_request_error", "email_taken", "an account with that email already exists")
		return
	case err != nil:
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	if err := s.Sessions.Start(r.Context(), w, u.ID); err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	e := repo.AuditEntry{ActorID: &u.ID, ActorEmail: u.Email, Action: "invite.accept", Resource: "users", ResourceID: u.ID, IP: auth.ClientIP(r, s.TrustedProxies)}
	s.Store.InsertAudit(r.Context(), e)
	api.WriteJSON(w, 201, u)
}
```

Note on `ErrConflict` in accept: if the email already exists, `createUser` fails inside the transaction, the transaction rolls back, and the invite stays usable.

`console/api/users.go`:
```go
package consoleapi

import (
	"net/http"

	"tollgate/console/auth"
	"tollgate/console/rbac"
	"tollgate/core/api"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

func (s *Server) listUsers(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	us, err := s.Store.ListUsers(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, us)
}

func (s *Server) updateUser(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id := r.PathValue("id")
	var in repo.UserUpdate
	if !decode(w, r, &in) {
		return
	}
	if in.Role != nil && !rbac.ValidRole(*in.Role) {
		badRequest(w, "role must be admin, member, or viewer")
		return
	}
	me := auth.UserFrom(r.Context())
	if id == me.ID && ((in.Role != nil && *in.Role != "admin") || (in.Disabled != nil && *in.Disabled)) {
		badRequest(w, "you can't demote or disable yourself")
		return
	}
	before, err := s.Store.GetUser(r.Context(), id)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	after, err := s.Store.UpdateUser(r.Context(), id, in)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "user.update", "users", id, before, after)
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, after)
}

// disableUser is the DELETE route: users are disabled, never removed, so their
// spend history stays attributable.
func (s *Server) disableUser(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id := r.PathValue("id")
	if id == auth.UserFrom(r.Context()).ID {
		badRequest(w, "you can't disable yourself")
		return
	}
	t := true
	u, err := s.Store.UpdateUser(r.Context(), id, repo.UserUpdate{Disabled: &t})
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "user.disable", "users", id, nil, u)
	w.WriteHeader(204)
}
```

package consoleapi

import (
	"net/http"

	"tollgate/console/auth"
	"tollgate/console/rbac"
	"tollgate/core/api"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

func (s *Server) listGroups(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	me := auth.UserFrom(r.Context())
	if !rbac.Has(me.Role, rbac.GroupsReadAll) {
		g, err := s.Store.GetGroup(r.Context(), me.GroupID)
		if err != nil {
			storeErr(w, err)
			return
		}
		api.WriteJSON(w, 200, []repo.Group{*g})
		return
	}
	gs, err := s.Store.ListGroups(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, gs)
}

func validGroup(w http.ResponseWriter, in repo.GroupInput) bool {
	switch {
	case in.Name == "":
		badRequest(w, "name is required")
	case !validBudget(in.BudgetType):
		badRequest(w, "budget_type must be unlimited, daily, monthly, or total")
	case in.BudgetNanos < 0:
		badRequest(w, "budget must not be negative")
	default:
		return true
	}
	return false
}

func (s *Server) createGroup(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var in repo.GroupInput
	if !decode(w, r, &in) || !validGroup(w, in) {
		return
	}
	g, err := s.Store.CreateGroup(r.Context(), in)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "group.create", "groups", g.ID, nil, g)
	api.WriteJSON(w, 201, g)
}

func (s *Server) updateGroup(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id := r.PathValue("id")
	var in repo.GroupInput
	if !decode(w, r, &in) || !validGroup(w, in) {
		return
	}
	before, err := s.Store.GetGroup(r.Context(), id)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	after, err := s.Store.UpdateGroup(r.Context(), id, in)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "group.update", "groups", id, before, after)
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, after)
}

`console/api/keys.go`:
```go
package consoleapi

import (
	"net/http"

	"tollgate/console/auth"
	"tollgate/console/rbac"
	"tollgate/core/api"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

func (s *Server) listKeys(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	me := auth.UserFrom(r.Context())
	owner := me.ID
	if rbac.Has(me.Role, rbac.KeysReadAll) {
		owner = r.URL.Query().Get("user_id") // "" = everyone
	}
	ks, err := s.Store.ListKeys(r.Context(), owner)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	if ks == nil {
		ks = []repo.APIKey{}
	}
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, ks)
}

func validKey(w http.ResponseWriter, in repo.KeyInput) bool {
	switch {
	case in.Alias == "":
		badRequest(w, "alias is required")
	case !validBudget(in.BudgetType):
		badRequest(w, "budget_type must be unlimited, daily, monthly, or total")
	case in.BudgetNanos < 0:
		badRequest(w, "budget must not be negative")
	default:
		return true
	}
	return false
}

func (s *Server) createKey(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var in repo.KeyInput
	if !decode(w, r, &in) || !validKey(w, in) {
		return
	}
	me := auth.UserFrom(r.Context())
	// Only admins choose the billing group; everyone else bills to their own.
	if !rbac.Has(me.Role, rbac.KeysWriteAll) || in.GroupID == "" {
		in.GroupID = me.GroupID
	}
	raw, k, err := s.Store.CreateKey(r.Context(), me.ID, in)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "key.create", "api_keys", k.ID, nil, k)
	api.WriteJSON(w, 201, map[string]any{"key": k, "raw": raw})
}

// ownedKey loads a key the caller may modify. Keys owned by someone else are
// reported as 404, not 403, so IDs can't be probed.
func (s *Server) ownedKey(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) (*repo.APIKey, bool) {
	k, err := s.Store.GetKey(r.Context(), r.PathValue("id"))
	me := auth.UserFrom(r.Context())
	if err != nil || (k.UserID != me.ID && !rbac.Has(me.Role, rbac.KeysWriteAll)) {
		notFound(w)
		return nil, false
	}
	return k, true
}

func (s *Server) updateKey(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	before, ok := s.ownedKey(w, r)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	var in repo.KeyInput
	if !decode(w, r, &in) || !validKey(w, in) {
		return
	}
	if !rbac.Has(auth.UserFrom(r.Context()).Role, rbac.KeysWriteAll) || in.GroupID == "" {
		in.GroupID = before.GroupID
	}
	after, err := s.Store.UpdateKey(r.Context(), before.ID, in)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "key.update", "api_keys", before.ID, before, after)
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, after)
}

func (s *Server) revokeKey(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	k, ok := s.ownedKey(w, r)
	if !ok {
		return
	}
	if err := s.Store.RevokeKey(r.Context(), k.ID); err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "key.revoke", "api_keys", k.ID, k, nil)
	w.WriteHeader(204)
}
```

| `GET …/audit?action&actor_id&limit&offset` → `{entries, total}` | `audit:read` |
| `GET …/settings`, `PUT …/settings` | `settings:read` / `settings:write` |

`console/api/admin_test.go`:
```go
package consoleapi

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

func TestCredentials_SecretNeverReturnedOrAudited(t *testing.T) {
	e := newTenv(t)
	w := e.as("admin@x.io", "POST", "/console/api/v1/credentials", map[string]string{"name": "openai-prod", "kind": "api_key", "secret": "sk-very-secret"})
	if w.Code != 201 || strings.Contains(w.Body.String(), "sk-very-secret") {
		t.Fatalf("%d %s", w.Code, w.Body)
	}
	if list := e.as("admin@x.io", "GET", "/console/api/v1/credentials", nil).Body.String(); strings.Contains(list, "sk-very") || strings.Contains(list, "ciphertext") {
		t.Fatalf("list leaks: %s", list)
	}
	entries, _, _ := e.store.ListAudit(context.Background(), repo.AuditFilter{Limit: 50})
	for _, a := range entries {
		if strings.Contains(string(a.Details), "sk-very") {
			t.Fatal("secret in audit log")
		}
	}
	if w := e.as("member@x.io", "GET", "/console/api/v1/credentials", nil); w.Code != 403 {
		t.Fatalf("member: %d", w.Code)
	}
}

func TestModels_WriteReloadsRegistryAndValidates(t *testing.T) {
	e := newTenv(t)
	body := map[string]any{"name": "gpt", "provider": "openai", "upstream_model": "gpt-4o", "enabled": true,
		"price_input_micros": 2500000, "price_output_micros": 10000000}
	w := e.as("admin@x.io", "POST", "/console/api/v1/models", body)
	if w.Code != 201 || e.reloads != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d reloads=%d %s", w.Code, e.reloads, w.Body)
	}
	body["provider"] = "cohere"
	body["name"] = "x"
	if w := e.as("admin@x.io", "POST", "/console/api/v1/models", body); w.Code != 400 {
		t.Fatalf("bad provider: %d", w.Code)
	}
	if w := e.as("viewer@x.io", "GET", "/console/api/v1/models", nil); w.Code != 200 {
		t.Fatalf("viewer list: %d", w.Code)
	}
	if w := e.as("member@x.io", "POST", "/console/api/v1/models", body); w.Code != 403 {
		t.Fatalf("member write: %d", w.Code)
	}
}

func TestModels_DeleteWithSpendHistoryIs409(t *testing.T) {
	e := newTenv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	m := decodeInto[repo.Model](t, e.as("admin@x.io", "POST", "/console/api/v1/models",
		map[string]any{"name": "m", "provider": "openai", "upstream_model": "m", "enabled": true}))
	_, k, _ := e.store.CreateKey(ctx, e.admin.ID, repo.KeyInput{Alias: "k", GroupID: e.group.ID, BudgetType: "unlimited"})
	e.store.RecordSpend(ctx, []repo.SpendRecord{{KeyID: k.ID, UserID: e.admin.ID, GroupID: e.group.ID, ModelID: m.ID, ModelName: "m",
		Provider: "openai", Status: "success", HTTPStatus: 200, CreatedAt: time.Now()}})
	if w := e.as("admin@x.io", "DELETE", "/console/api/v1/models/"+m.ID, nil); w.Code != 409 {
		t.Fatalf("%d", w.Code)
	}
}

func TestSpend_MemberSeesOnlyOwn(t *testing.T) {
	e := newTenv(t)
	ctx := context.Background()
	m, _ := e.store.CreateModel(ctx, repo.ModelInput{Name: "m", Provider: "openai", UpstreamModel: "m", Enabled: true, Extra: json.RawMessage(`{}`)})
	var recs []repo.SpendRecord
	for _, u := range []*repo.User{e.admin, e.member} {
		_, k, _ := e.store.CreateKey(ctx, u.ID, repo.KeyInput{Alias: "k", GroupID: e.group.ID, BudgetType: "unlimited"})
		recs = append(recs, repo.SpendRecord{KeyID: k.ID, UserID: u.ID, GroupID: e.group.ID, ModelID: m.ID, ModelName: "m",
			Provider: "openai", CostNanos: 100, Status: "success", HTTPStatus: 200, CreatedAt: time.Now()})
	}
	e.store.RecordSpend(ctx, recs)
	type out struct {
		Rows   []repo.SpendRow
		Recent []repo.SpendLog
	}
	// member tries to widen the scope with user_id; it must be ignored
	got := decodeInto[out](t, e.as("member@x.io", "GET", "/console/api/v1/spend?group_by=user&user_id="+e.admin.ID, nil))
	if len(got.Rows) != 1 || got.Rows[0].Bucket != e.member.ID || len(got.Recent) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("member: %+v", got)
	}
	all := decodeInto[out](t, e.as("admin@x.io", "GET", "/console/api/v1/spend?group_by=user", nil))
	if len(all.Rows) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("admin: %+v", all)
	}
}

func TestSettingsAndAudit_AdminOnly(t *testing.T) {
	e := newTenv(t)
	if w := e.as("member@x.io", "GET", "/console/api/v1/settings", nil); w.Code != 403 {
		t.Fatalf("member settings: %d", w.Code)
	}
	w := e.as("admin@x.io", "PUT", "/console/api/v1/settings", map[string]string{"server_name": "Mine", "timezone": "Asia/Taipei", "currency": "USD"})
	if w.Code != 200 {
		t.Fatalf("%d %s", w.Code, w.Body)
	}
	if w := e.as("admin@x.io", "PUT", "/console/api/v1/settings", map[string]string{"server_name": "Mine", "timezone": "Nowhere/Land", "currency": "USD"}); w.Code != 400 {
		t.Fatalf("bad tz: %d", w.Code)
	}
	a := decodeInto[struct {
		Entries []repo.AuditEntry
		Total   int
	}](t, e.as("admin@x.io", "GET", "/console/api/v1/audit?action=settings.update", nil))
	if a.Total != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("audit total=%d", a.Total)
	}
	if w := e.as("member@x.io", "GET", "/console/api/v1/audit", nil); w.Code != 403 {
		t.Fatalf("member audit: %d", w.Code)
	}
}
```

package consoleapi

import (
	"net/http"

	"tollgate/console/rbac"
)

func (s *Server) extraRoutes() map[string]http.Handler {
	req := s.Sessions.Require
	return map[string]http.Handler{
		"GET " + p + "/credentials":         req(rbac.ModelsWrite, s.listCredentials),
		"POST " + p + "/credentials":        req(rbac.ModelsWrite, s.createCredential),
		"DELETE " + p + "/credentials/{id}": req(rbac.ModelsWrite, s.deleteCredential),
		"GET " + p + "/models":              req(rbac.ModelsRead, s.listModels),
		"POST " + p + "/models":             req(rbac.ModelsWrite, s.createModel),
		"PUT " + p + "/models/{id}":         req(rbac.ModelsWrite, s.updateModel),
		"DELETE " + p + "/models/{id}":      req(rbac.ModelsWrite, s.deleteModel),
		"GET " + p + "/spend":               req(rbac.SpendReadOwn, s.spend),
		"GET " + p + "/audit":               req(rbac.AuditRead, s.auditLog),
		"GET " + p + "/settings":            req(rbac.SettingsRead, s.getSettings),
		"PUT " + p + "/settings":            req(rbac.SettingsWrite, s.putSettings),
	}
}

`console/api/credentials.go`:
```go
package consoleapi

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"

	"tollgate/core/api"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

func (s *Server) listCredentials(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	cs, err := s.Store.ListCredentials(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	if cs == nil {
		cs = []repo.Credential{}
	}
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, cs) // Ciphertext/Nonce are json:"-"
}

func (s *Server) createCredential(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var in struct{ Name, Kind, Secret string }
	if !decode(w, r, &in) {
		return
	}
	switch {
	case in.Name == "" || in.Secret == "":
		badRequest(w, "name and secret are required")
		return
	case in.Kind != "api_key" && in.Kind != "gcp_service_account":
		badRequest(w, "kind must be api_key or gcp_service_account")
		return
	case in.Kind == "gcp_service_account" && !json.Valid([]byte(in.Secret)):
		badRequest(w, "service account secret must be the JSON key file contents")
		return
	}
	nonce, ct, err := s.Vault.Seal(in.Name, []byte(in.Secret))
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	c, err := s.Store.CreateCredential(r.Context(), in.Name, in.Kind, nonce, ct)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "credential.create", "provider_credentials", c.ID, nil, c)
	api.WriteJSON(w, 201, c)
}

func (s *Server) deleteCredential(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id := r.PathValue("id")
	if err := s.Store.DeleteCredential(r.Context(), id); err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err) // ErrConflict → 409 while models still use it
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "credential.delete", "provider_credentials", id, nil, nil)
	w.WriteHeader(204)
}
```

`console/api/models.go`:
```go
package consoleapi

import (
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"

	"tollgate/core/api"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

func (s *Server) listModels(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	ms, err := s.Store.ListModels(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	if ms == nil {
		ms = []repo.Model{}
	}
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, ms)
}

func validModel(w http.ResponseWriter, in repo.ModelInput) bool {
	switch {
	case in.Name == "" || in.UpstreamModel == "":
		badRequest(w, "name and upstream_model are required")
	case in.Provider != "openai" && in.Provider != "anthropic" && in.Provider != "google":
		badRequest(w, "provider must be openai, anthropic, or google")
	case in.PriceInputMicros < 0 || in.PriceOutputMicros < 0:
		badRequest(w, "prices must not be negative")
	case in.Provider != "openai" && in.CredentialID == nil:
		badRequest(w, in.Provider+" models need a credential")
	default:
		return true
	}
	return false
}

func (s *Server) reload(r *http.Request) {
	if err := s.Reload(r.Context()); err != nil {
		slog.Error("model registry reload failed", "err", err)
	}
}

func (s *Server) createModel(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var in repo.ModelInput
	if !decode(w, r, &in) || !validModel(w, in) {
		return
	}
	m, err := s.Store.CreateModel(r.Context(), in)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "model.create", "models", m.ID, nil, m)
	s.reload(r)
	api.WriteJSON(w, 201, m)
}

func (s *Server) updateModel(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id := r.PathValue("id")
	var in repo.ModelInput
	if !decode(w, r, &in) || !validModel(w, in) {
		return
	}
	before, err := s.Store.GetModel(r.Context(), id)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	after, err := s.Store.UpdateModel(r.Context(), id, in)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "model.update", "models", id, before, after)
	s.reload(r)
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, after)
}

func (s *Server) deleteModel(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id := r.PathValue("id")
	err := s.Store.DeleteModel(r.Context(), id)
	if errors.Is(err, repo.ErrConflict) {
		api.WriteError(w, 409, "invalid_request_error", "model_in_use", "model has spend history; disable it instead")
		return
	}
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "model.delete", "models", id, nil, nil)
	s.reload(r)
	w.WriteHeader(204)
}
```

`console/api/spend.go`:
```go
package consoleapi

import (
	"net/http"
	"time"

	"tollgate/console/auth"
	"tollgate/console/rbac"
	"tollgate/core/api"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

func (s *Server) spend(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	q := r.URL.Query()
	now := time.Now()
	f := repo.SpendFilter{From: now.AddDate(0, 0, -30), To: now.Add(time.Minute), GroupBy: q.Get("group_by"),
		UserID: q.Get("user_id"), GroupID: q.Get("group_id"), KeyID: q.Get("key_id")}
	if f.GroupBy == "" {
		f.GroupBy = "day"
	}
	for name, dst := range map[string]*time.Time{"from": &f.From, "to": &f.To} {
		if v := q.Get(name); v != "" {
			t, err := time.Parse(time.RFC3339, v)
			if err != nil {
				badRequest(w, name+" must be RFC3339")
				return
			}
			*dst = t
		}
	}
	me := auth.UserFrom(r.Context())
	if !rbac.Has(me.Role, rbac.SpendReadAll) {
		f.UserID, f.GroupID = me.ID, ""
	}
	rows, err := s.Store.QuerySpend(r.Context(), f)
	if err != nil {
		badRequest(w, err.Error())
		return
	}
	recent, err := s.Store.RecentSpend(r.Context(), f, 50)
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	if rows == nil {
		rows = []repo.SpendRow{}
	}
	if recent == nil {
		recent = []repo.SpendLog{}
	}
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, map[string]any{"rows": rows, "recent": recent})
}
```

package consoleapi

import (
	"net/http"
	"strconv"

	"tollgate/core/api"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

func (s *Server) auditLog(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	q := r.URL.Query()
	limit, _ := strconv.Atoi(q.Get("limit"))
	offset, _ := strconv.Atoi(q.Get("offset"))
	entries, total, err := s.Store.ListAudit(r.Context(), repo.AuditFilter{ActorID: q.Get("actor_id"), Action: q.Get("action"), Limit: limit, Offset: offset})
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	if entries == nil {
		entries = []repo.AuditEntry{}
	}
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, map[string]any{"entries": entries, "total": total})
}

```go
package consoleapi

import (
	"net/http"

	"tollgate/core/api"
	"tollgate/core/db/repo"
)

func (s *Server) getSettings(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	st, err := s.Store.GetSettings(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		storeErr(w, err)
		return
	}
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, st)
}

func (s *Server) putSettings(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var in repo.Settings
	if !decode(w, r, &in) {
		return
	}
	if in.ServerName == "" || in.Currency == "" {
		badRequest(w, "server_name and currency are required")
		return
	}
	before, _ := s.Store.GetSettings(r.Context())
	after, err := s.Store.UpdateSettings(r.Context(), in)
	if err != nil {
		badRequest(w, err.Error()) // only failure mode besides DB down is an invalid timezone
		return
	}
	s.audit(r, "settings.update", "server_settings", "1", before, after)
	api.WriteJSON(w, 200, after)
}

kill %1; rm /tmp/tg.jar

- Produces (Go): `web.Dist() fs.FS`, `web.Handler(fsys fs.FS) http.Handler`
- Produces (TS): `api<T>(path, {method?, body?}): Promise<T>`, `ApiError`, money helpers `usd`, `toNanos`, `fromNanos`, `toMicros`, `fromMicros`; `useAuth(): {me, can, refresh, logout}`; `RequireAuth`, `Gate`; UI kit `Button`, `Field`, `inputCls`, `Modal`, `Table`, `Badge`, `ErrorText`, `PageHeader`, `useLoad<T>(path | null)`, `CopyBox`

package web

import (
	"net/http/httptest"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"testing/fstest"
)

func TestHandler(t *testing.T) {
	fsys := fstest.MapFS{
		"index.html":    {Data: []byte("<html>app</html>")},
		"assets/app.js": {Data: []byte("js")},
	}
	h := Handler(fsys)
	get := func(path string) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
		w := httptest.NewRecorder()
		h.ServeHTTP(w, httptest.NewRequest("GET", path, nil))
		return w
	}
	if w := get("/assets/app.js"); w.Body.String() != "js" || !strings.Contains(w.Header().Get("Cache-Control"), "immutable") {
		t.Fatalf("asset: %q %v", w.Body, w.Header())
	}
	if w := get("/keys"); w.Body.String() != "<html>app</html>" || w.Header().Get("X-Frame-Options") != "DENY" {
		t.Fatalf("spa fallback: %q", w.Body)
	}
	if w := get("/invite/abc"); w.Body.String() != "<html>app</html>" {
		t.Fatal("invite page must be the SPA")
	}
	if w := get("/console/api/v1/nope"); w.Code != 404 || strings.Contains(w.Body.String(), "<html>") {
		t.Fatalf("unknown API path served HTML: %d", w.Code)
	}
	if w := Handler(fstest.MapFS{}); w != nil {
		rec := httptest.NewRecorder()
		w.ServeHTTP(rec, httptest.NewRequest("GET", "/", nil))
		if rec.Code != 503 {
			t.Fatalf("unbuilt console: %d", rec.Code)
		}
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `web/embed.go`**

```go
// Package web embeds the built console SPA.
package web

import (
	"embed"
	"io/fs"
	"net/http"
	"path"
	"strings"

	"tollgate/core/api"
)

//go:embed all:dist
var dist embed.FS

func Dist() fs.FS {
	sub, _ := fs.Sub(dist, "dist")
	return sub
}

// Handler serves static files and falls back to index.html for client-side routes.
func Handler(fsys fs.FS) http.Handler {
	files := http.FileServerFS(fsys)
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		h := w.Header()
		h.Set("X-Frame-Options", "DENY")
		h.Set("X-Content-Type-Options", "nosniff")
		h.Set("Referrer-Policy", "same-origin")

		p := strings.TrimPrefix(path.Clean(r.URL.Path), "/")
		if p != "" && p != "index.html" {
			if st, err := fs.Stat(fsys, p); err == nil && !st.IsDir() {
				if strings.HasPrefix(p, "assets/") {
					h.Set("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=31536000, immutable")
				}
				files.ServeHTTP(w, r)
				return
			}
		}
		for _, prefix := range []string{"/v1/", "/auth/", "/console/"} {
			if strings.HasPrefix(r.URL.Path, prefix) {
				api.WriteError(w, 404, "invalid_request_error", "not_found", "no such endpoint")
				return
			}
		}
		index, err := fs.ReadFile(fsys, "index.html")
		if err != nil {
			http.Error(w, "console not built: run `make web`", http.StatusServiceUnavailable)
			return
		}
		h.Set("Content-Type", "text/html; charset=utf-8")
		h.Set("Cache-Control", "no-cache")
		w.Write(index)
	})
}
```

```ts
import { defineConfig } from 'vite'
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite'

// `npm run dev` serves the SPA on :5173 and proxies API calls to the Go server on :4000.
// Start the Go server with BASE_URL=http://localhost:5173: the browser's Origin header is
// :5173, and the CSRF check rejects state-changing requests whose Origin isn't BASE_URL.
export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
  server: {
    proxy: {
      '/auth': 'http://localhost:4000',
      '/console': 'http://localhost:4000',
      '/v1': 'http://localhost:4000',
    },
  },
})
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `api.ts` and `types.ts`**

`web/src/api.ts`:
```ts
export class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(public status: number, message: string) {
    super(message)
  }
}

export async function api<T>(path: string, opts: { method?: string; body?: unknown } = {}): Promise<T> {
  const res = await fetch(path, {
    method: opts.method ?? 'GET',
    credentials: 'same-origin',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, // also satisfies the server's CSRF check
    body: opts.body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(opts.body),
  })
  if (res.status === 401 && !path.startsWith('/auth/')) {
    window.location.assign('/login')
    throw new ApiError(401, 'signed out')
  }
  const text = await res.text()
  const data = text ? JSON.parse(text) : null
  if (!res.ok) throw new ApiError(res.status, data?.error?.message ?? res.statusText)
  return data as T
}

// Money: spend/budgets are nano-USD, prices are micro-USD per 1M tokens.
export const usd = (nanos: number) => {
  const d = nanos / 1e9
  return '$' + (d !== 0 && Math.abs(d) < 0.01 ? d.toFixed(6) : d.toFixed(2))
}
export const toNanos = (dollars: string) => Math.round(parseFloat(dollars || '0') * 1e9)
export const fromNanos = (nanos: number) => String(nanos / 1e9)
export const toMicros = (dollars: string) => Math.round(parseFloat(dollars || '0') * 1e6)
export const fromMicros = (micros: number) => String(micros / 1e6)
export const num = (s: string): number | null => (s.trim() === '' ? null : Number(s))
export const when = (iso: string | null) => (iso ? new Date(iso).toLocaleString() : '—')
```

export type BudgetType = 'unlimited' | 'daily' | 'monthly' | 'total'

  tpm_limit: number | null
}

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `auth.tsx` and `components/ui.tsx`**

`web/src/auth.tsx`:
```tsx
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { Navigate, useLocation } from 'react-router-dom'
import { api } from './api'
import type { Me } from './types'

interface AuthCtx {
  me: Me | null
  can: (perm: string) => boolean
  refresh: () => Promise<void>
  logout: () => Promise<void>
}

const Ctx = createContext<AuthCtx | null>(null)

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [me, setMe] = useState<Me | null | undefined>(undefined)
  const refresh = useCallback(() => api<Me>('/auth/me').then(setMe, () => setMe(null)), [])
  useEffect(() => {
    refresh()
  }, [refresh])
  const logout = async () => {
    await api('/auth/logout', { method: 'POST' })
    setMe(null)
  }
  if (me === undefined) return null
  const can = (perm: string) => !!me?.permissions.includes(perm)
  return <Ctx.Provider value={{ me, can, refresh, logout }}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>
}

export function useAuth() {
  const c = useContext(Ctx)
  if (!c) throw new Error('useAuth outside AuthProvider')
  return c
}

export function RequireAuth({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const { me } = useAuth()
  const loc = useLocation()
  if (!me) return <Navigate to="/login" replace state={{ from: loc.pathname }} />
  return <>{children}</>
}

// Gate hides a page the user lacks permission for. The server enforces the same rule.
export function Gate({ perm, children }: { perm: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { can } = useAuth()
  if (!can(perm)) return <p className="p-8 text-zinc-500">You don't have access to this page.</p>
  return <>{children}</>
}
```

`web/src/components/ui.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState, type ButtonHTMLAttributes, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { api } from '../api'

export const inputCls =
  'mt-1 block w-full rounded-md border border-zinc-300 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm focus:border-zinc-900 focus:outline-none'

export function Button({ variant = 'primary', className = '', ...props }: ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> & { variant?: 'primary' | 'ghost' | 'danger' }) {
  const styles = {
    primary: 'bg-zinc-900 text-white hover:bg-zinc-700',
    ghost: 'border border-zinc-300 bg-white hover:bg-zinc-100',
    danger: 'bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-500',
  }[variant]
  return <button className={`rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium disabled:opacity-50 ${styles} ${className}`} {...props} />
}

export function Field({ label, hint, children }: { label: string; hint?: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label className="block text-sm">
      <span className="font-medium text-zinc-700">{label}</span>
      {children}
      {hint && <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-zinc-500">{hint}</span>}
    </label>
  )
}

export function Modal({ title, onClose, children }: { title: string; onClose: () => void; children: ReactNode }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => e.key === 'Escape' && onClose()
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
  }, [onClose])
  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-10 flex items-start justify-center overflow-y-auto bg-black/40 p-4 pt-16" onClick={onClose}>
      <div role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={title} className="w-full max-w-lg rounded-lg bg-white p-6 shadow-xl" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-semibold">{title}</h2>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export interface Col<T> {
  head: string
  cell: (row: T) => ReactNode
}

export function Table<T>({ rows, cols, empty = 'Nothing here yet.' }: { rows: T[]; cols: Col<T>[]; empty?: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white">
      <table className="min-w-full text-sm">
        <thead className="bg-zinc-50 text-left text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">
          <tr>{cols.map((c) => <th key={c.head} className="px-4 py-2 font-medium">{c.head}</th>)}</tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody className="divide-y divide-zinc-100">
          {rows.length === 0 && (
            <tr>
              <td colSpan={cols.length} className="px-4 py-6 text-center text-zinc-500">{empty}</td>
            </tr>
          )}
          {rows.map((r, i) => (
            <tr key={i}>{cols.map((c) => <td key={c.head} className="whitespace-nowrap px-4 py-2">{c.cell(r)}</td>)}</tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  )
}

export function Badge({ tone = 'zinc', children }: { tone?: 'zinc' | 'green' | 'red' | 'amber'; children: ReactNode }) {
  const t = { zinc: 'bg-zinc-100 text-zinc-700', green: 'bg-green-100 text-green-800', red: 'bg-red-100 text-red-800', amber: 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-800' }[tone]
  return <span className={`rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${t}`}>{children}</span>
}

export function ErrorText({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  if (!children) return null
  return <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-600">{children}</p>
}

export function PageHeader({ title, children }: { title: string; children?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold">{title}</h1>
      <div className="flex gap-2">{children}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function CopyBox({ value }: { value: string }) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)
  return (
    <div className="flex gap-2">
      <input readOnly value={value} className={inputCls + ' font-mono'} onFocus={(e) => e.target.select()} />
      <Button type="button" variant="ghost" className="mt-1" onClick={() => navigator.clipboard.writeText(value).then(() => setCopied(true))}>
        {copied ? 'Copied' : 'Copy'}
      </Button>
    </div>
  )
}

export function useLoad<T>(path: string | null) {
  const [data, setData] = useState<T | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const reload = useCallback(() => {
    if (!path) return
    api<T>(path).then((d) => { setData(d); setError('') }, (e: Error) => setError(e.message))
  }, [path])
  useEffect(reload, [reload])
  return { data, error, reload }
}
```

import { NavLink, Outlet, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { useAuth } from './auth'

// One layout for every role; nav items are filtered by permission, so members
// and viewers get the "user view" and admins see everything.
const nav = [
  { to: '/', label: 'Dashboard', perm: 'spend:read_own' },
  { to: '/keys', label: 'API keys', perm: 'keys:read_own' },
  { to: '/models', label: 'Models', perm: 'models:read' },
  { to: '/groups', label: 'Groups', perm: 'groups:read_own' },
  { to: '/users', label: 'Users', perm: 'users:read' },
  { to: '/invites', label: 'Invites', perm: 'invites:read' },
  { to: '/credentials', label: 'Credentials', perm: 'models:write' },
  { to: '/audit', label: 'Audit log', perm: 'audit:read' },
  { to: '/settings', label: 'Settings', perm: 'settings:read' },
]

export default function Layout() {
  const { me, can, logout } = useAuth()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  if (!me) return null
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col md:flex-row">
      <aside className="border-b border-zinc-200 bg-white md:w-56 md:border-b-0 md:border-r">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-4 text-lg font-semibold">
          <span aria-hidden>{me.server.server_icon || '⛩️'}</span>
          {me.server.server_name}
        </div>
        <nav className="flex gap-1 overflow-x-auto px-2 pb-2 md:flex-col md:pb-4">
          {nav.filter((n) => can(n.perm)).map((n) => (
            <NavLink key={n.to} to={n.to} end={n.to === '/'}
              className={({ isActive }) => `whitespace-nowrap rounded-md px-3 py-2 text-sm ${isActive ? 'bg-zinc-900 text-white' : 'text-zinc-700 hover:bg-zinc-100'}`}>
              {n.label}
            </NavLink>
          ))}
        </nav>
        <div className="hidden border-t border-zinc-200 px-4 py-3 text-xs text-zinc-500 md:block">
          <div className="font-medium text-zinc-800">{me.user.name}</div>
          <div>{me.user.email} · {me.user.role}</div>
          <button className="mt-2 underline" onClick={() => logout().then(() => navigate('/login'))}>Sign out</button>
        </div>
      </aside>
      <main className="flex-1 p-4 md:p-8">
        <Outlet />
      </main>
    </div>
  )
}

`web/src/pages/Login.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { Navigate, useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom'
import { api } from '../api'
import { useAuth } from '../auth'
import { Button, ErrorText, Field, inputCls } from '../components/ui'

export default function Login() {
  const { me, refresh } = useAuth()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const from = (useLocation().state as { from?: string } | null)?.from ?? '/'
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  if (me) return <Navigate to={from} replace />

  const submit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setBusy(true)
    setError('')
    try {
      await api('/auth/login', { method: 'POST', body: { email, password } })
      await refresh()
      navigate(from, { replace: true })
    } catch (err) {
      setError((err as Error).message)
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center p-4">
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-4 rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-6">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Sign in</h1>
        <Field label="Email"><input className={inputCls} type="email" autoComplete="username" required value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} /></Field>
        <Field label="Password"><input className={inputCls} type="password" autoComplete="current-password" required value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} /></Field>
        <ErrorText>{error}</ErrorText>
        <Button className="w-full" disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Signing in…' : 'Sign in'}</Button>
      </form>
    </div>
  )
}
```

`web/src/pages/Invite.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom'
import { api } from '../api'
import { useAuth } from '../auth'
import { Button, ErrorText, Field, inputCls } from '../components/ui'

interface InviteInfo { role: string; group_name: string; email: string; status: string }

export default function Invite() {
  const { token = '' } = useParams()
  const { refresh } = useAuth()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const [info, setInfo] = useState<InviteInfo | null>(null)
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState('')
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ email: '', name: '', password: '', confirm: '' })
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  useEffect(() => {
    api<InviteInfo>(`/auth/invite/${encodeURIComponent(token)}`).then(
      (i) => { setInfo(i); setForm((f) => ({ ...f, email: i.email })) },
      () => setLoadError('This invite link is not valid.'),
    )
  }, [token])

  const submit = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (form.password !== form.confirm) return setError('Passwords do not match.')
    if (form.password.length < 12) return setError('Password must be at least 12 characters.')
    try {
      await api(`/auth/invite/${encodeURIComponent(token)}`, { method: 'POST', body: { email: form.email, name: form.name, password: form.password } })
      await refresh()
      navigate('/', { replace: true })
    } catch (err) {
      setError((err as Error).message)
    }
  }

  const set = (k: keyof typeof form) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => setForm({ ...form, [k]: e.target.value })
  const unusable = loadError || (info && info.status !== 'active')
  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="w-full max-w-sm rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-6">
        {unusable ? (
          <p className="text-zinc-700">{loadError || `This invite has been ${info?.status}. Ask an admin for a new link.`}</p>
        ) : !info ? null : (
          <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-4">
            <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Create your account</h1>
            <p className="text-sm text-zinc-600">You're joining <b>{info.group_name}</b> as <b>{info.role}</b>.</p>
            <Field label="Email"><input className={inputCls} type="email" required readOnly={!!info.email} value={form.email} onChange={set('email')} /></Field>
            <Field label="Name"><input className={inputCls} required value={form.name} onChange={set('name')} /></Field>
            <Field label="Password" hint="At least 12 characters."><input className={inputCls} type="password" autoComplete="new-password" required value={form.password} onChange={set('password')} /></Field>
            <Field label="Confirm password"><input className={inputCls} type="password" autoComplete="new-password" required value={form.confirm} onChange={set('confirm')} /></Field>
            <ErrorText>{error}</ErrorText>
            <Button className="w-full">Create account</Button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

`web/src/App.tsx` (first version; Task 18 replaces it with all routes):
```tsx
import { BrowserRouter, Navigate, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom'
import { AuthProvider, RequireAuth, useAuth } from './auth'
import Layout from './Layout'
import Invite from './pages/Invite'
import Login from './pages/Login'

function Home() {
  const { me } = useAuth()
  return <p>Signed in as {me?.user.email}.</p>
}

export default function App() {
  return (
    <BrowserRouter>
      <AuthProvider>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />
          <Route path="/invite/:token" element={<Invite />} />
          <Route element={<RequireAuth><Layout /></RequireAuth>}>
            <Route index element={<Home />} />
          </Route>
          <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
        </Routes>
      </AuthProvider>
    </BrowserRouter>
  )
}
```

`web/src/components/LimitsFields.tsx`:
```tsx
import { fromNanos, num, toNanos } from '../api'
import type { BudgetType, Limits } from '../types'
import { Field, inputCls } from './ui'

export interface LimitsState { budget_type: BudgetType; budget: string; rpm: string; tpm: string }

export const limitsState = (l?: Partial<Limits>): LimitsState => ({
  budget_type: l?.budget_type ?? 'unlimited',
  budget: l?.budget_nanos ? fromNanos(l.budget_nanos) : '',
  rpm: l?.rpm_limit?.toString() ?? '',
  tpm: l?.tpm_limit?.toString() ?? '',
})

export const limitsPayload = (s: LimitsState) => ({
  budget_type: s.budget_type,
  budget_nanos: s.budget_type === 'unlimited' ? 0 : toNanos(s.budget),
  rpm_limit: num(s.rpm),
  tpm_limit: num(s.tpm),
})

export function LimitsFields({ value, onChange }: { value: LimitsState; onChange: (v: LimitsState) => void }) {
  const set = (k: keyof LimitsState) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => onChange({ ...value, [k]: e.target.value })
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
      <Field label="Budget">
        <select className={inputCls} value={value.budget_type} onChange={set('budget_type')}>
          <option value="unlimited">Unlimited</option>
          <option value="daily">Daily</option>
          <option value="monthly">Monthly</option>
          <option value="total">Total (never resets)</option>
        </select>
      </Field>
      <Field label="Budget amount (USD)">
        <input className={inputCls} type="number" min="0" step="0.01" disabled={value.budget_type === 'unlimited'} value={value.budget} onChange={set('budget')} />
      </Field>
      <Field label="Requests / minute" hint="Blank = no limit">
        <input className={inputCls} type="number" min="1" value={value.rpm} onChange={set('rpm')} />
      </Field>
      <Field label="Tokens / minute" hint="Blank = no limit">
        <input className={inputCls} type="number" min="1" value={value.tpm} onChange={set('tpm')} />
      </Field>
    </div>
  )
}

export function budgetText(l: Limits) {
  if (l.budget_type === 'unlimited') return 'Unlimited'
  return `${(l.spend_nanos / 1e9).toFixed(2)} / ${(l.budget_nanos / 1e9).toFixed(2)} ${l.budget_type}`
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Keys page**

`web/src/pages/Keys.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { api, when } from '../api'
import { useAuth } from '../auth'
import { budgetText, LimitsFields, limitsPayload, limitsState, type LimitsState } from '../components/LimitsFields'
import { Badge, Button, CopyBox, ErrorText, Field, inputCls, Modal, PageHeader, Table, useLoad } from '../components/ui'
import type { ApiKey, Group, User } from '../types'

interface Form { alias: string; models: string; expires: string; group_id: string; limits: LimitsState }

const emptyForm = (k?: ApiKey): Form => ({
  alias: k?.alias ?? '',
  models: k?.models.join(', ') ?? '',
  expires: k?.expires_at?.slice(0, 10) ?? '',
  group_id: k?.group_id ?? '',
  limits: limitsState(k),
})

export default function Keys() {
  const { can } = useAuth()
  const keys = useLoad<ApiKey[]>('/console/api/v1/keys')
  const users = useLoad<User[]>(can('users:read') ? '/console/api/v1/users' : null)
  const groups = useLoad<Group[]>('/console/api/v1/groups')
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<ApiKey | 'new' | null>(null)
  const [form, setForm] = useState<Form>(emptyForm())
  const [raw, setRaw] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const email = (id: string) => users.data?.find((u) => u.id === id)?.email ?? id.slice(0, 8)
  const groupName = (id: string) => groups.data?.find((g) => g.id === id)?.name ?? id.slice(0, 8)
  const open = (k: ApiKey | 'new') => { setEditing(k); setForm(emptyForm(k === 'new' ? undefined : k)); setError('') }

  const save = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    const body = {
      alias: form.alias,
      models: form.models.split(',').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean),
      expires_at: form.expires ? new Date(form.expires + 'T23:59:59').toISOString() : null,
      group_id: form.group_id || undefined,
      ...limitsPayload(form.limits),
    }
    try {
      if (editing === 'new') {
        const res = await api<{ raw: string }>('/console/api/v1/keys', { method: 'POST', body })
        setRaw(res.raw)
      } else if (editing) {
        await api(`/console/api/v1/keys/${editing.id}`, { method: 'PUT', body })
      }
      setEditing(null)
      keys.reload()
    } catch (err) {
      setError((err as Error).message)
    }
  }

  const revoke = async (k: ApiKey) => {
    if (!confirm(`Revoke "${k.alias}"? Clients using it will stop working immediately.`)) return
    await api(`/console/api/v1/keys/${k.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }).catch((e: Error) => alert(e.message))
    keys.reload()
  }

  const writable = can('keys:write_own')
  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="API keys">{writable && <Button onClick={() => open('new')}>New key</Button>}</PageHeader>
      <ErrorText>{keys.error}</ErrorText>
      <Table rows={keys.data ?? []} cols={[
        { head: 'Alias', cell: (k) => <span className="font-medium">{k.alias}</span> },
        { head: 'Key', cell: (k) => <code className="text-xs">{k.key_prefix}…</code> },
        ...(can('keys:read_all') ? [{ head: 'Owner', cell: (k: ApiKey) => email(k.user_id) }] : []),
        { head: 'Group', cell: (k) => groupName(k.group_id) },
        { head: 'Budget', cell: (k) => budgetText(k) },
        { head: 'RPM / TPM', cell: (k) => `${k.rpm_limit ?? '∞'} / ${k.tpm_limit ?? '∞'}` },
        { head: 'Last used', cell: (k) => when(k.last_used_at) },
        { head: 'Status', cell: (k) => (k.revoked_at ? <Badge tone="red">revoked</Badge> : k.expires_at && new Date(k.expires_at) < new Date() ? <Badge tone="amber">expired</Badge> : <Badge tone="green">active</Badge>) },
        { head: '', cell: (k) => writable && !k.revoked_at && (
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => open(k)}>Edit</Button>
            <Button variant="danger" onClick={() => revoke(k)}>Revoke</Button>
          </div>
        ) },
      ]} />

      {editing && (
        <Modal title={editing === 'new' ? 'New API key' : `Edit ${editing.alias}`} onClose={() => setEditing(null)}>
          <form onSubmit={save} className="space-y-4">
            <Field label="Alias"><input className={inputCls} required value={form.alias} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, alias: e.target.value })} /></Field>
            <Field label="Allowed models" hint="Comma-separated model names. Blank = all models.">
              <input className={inputCls} value={form.models} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, models: e.target.value })} />
            </Field>
            {can('keys:write_all') && (
              <Field label="Billing group">
                <select className={inputCls} value={form.group_id} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, group_id: e.target.value })}>
                  <option value="">Owner's group</option>
                  {groups.data?.map((g) => <option key={g.id} value={g.id}>{g.name}</option>)}
                </select>
              </Field>
            )}
            <LimitsFields value={form.limits} onChange={(limits) => setForm({ ...form, limits })} />
            <Field label="Expires on" hint="Blank = never"><input className={inputCls} type="date" value={form.expires} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, expires: e.target.value })} /></Field>
            <ErrorText>{error}</ErrorText>
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
              <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>Cancel</Button>
              <Button>Save</Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}

      {raw && (
        <Modal title="Your new API key" onClose={() => setRaw('')}>
          <p className="mb-3 text-sm text-zinc-600">Copy it now. It won't be shown again.</p>
          <CopyBox value={raw} />
          <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end"><Button onClick={() => setRaw('')}>Done</Button></div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Models and Credentials pages**

`web/src/pages/Models.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { api, fromMicros, toMicros } from '../api'
import { useAuth } from '../auth'
import { Badge, Button, ErrorText, Field, inputCls, Modal, PageHeader, Table, useLoad } from '../components/ui'
import type { Credential, Model } from '../types'

interface Form {
  name: string; provider: Model['provider']; upstream_model: string; api_base: string; credential_id: string; enabled: boolean
  price_in: string; price_out: string; price_cr: string; price_cw: string; extra: string
}

const toForm = (m?: Model): Form => ({
  name: m?.name ?? '', provider: m?.provider ?? 'openai', upstream_model: m?.upstream_model ?? '', api_base: m?.api_base ?? '',
  credential_id: m?.credential_id ?? '', enabled: m?.enabled ?? true,
  price_in: m ? fromMicros(m.price_input_micros) : '', price_out: m ? fromMicros(m.price_output_micros) : '',
  price_cr: m?.price_cache_read_micros != null ? fromMicros(m.price_cache_read_micros) : '',
  price_cw: m?.price_cache_write_micros != null ? fromMicros(m.price_cache_write_micros) : '',
  extra: JSON.stringify(m?.extra ?? {}, null, 2),
})

const optMicros = (s: string) => (s.trim() === '' ? null : toMicros(s))

export default function Models() {
  const { can } = useAuth()
  const writable = can('models:write')
  const models = useLoad<Model[]>('/console/api/v1/models')
  const creds = useLoad<Credential[]>(writable ? '/console/api/v1/credentials' : null)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Model | 'new' | null>(null)
  const [form, setForm] = useState<Form>(toForm())
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const open = (m: Model | 'new') => { setEditing(m); setForm(toForm(m === 'new' ? undefined : m)); setError('') }
  const set = (k: keyof Form) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement | HTMLTextAreaElement>) =>
    setForm({ ...form, [k]: e.target.type === 'checkbox' ? (e.target as HTMLInputElement).checked : e.target.value })

  const payload = (f: Form) => ({
    name: f.name, provider: f.provider, upstream_model: f.upstream_model, api_base: f.api_base,
    credential_id: f.credential_id || null, enabled: f.enabled,
    price_input_micros: toMicros(f.price_in), price_output_micros: toMicros(f.price_out),
    price_cache_read_micros: optMicros(f.price_cr), price_cache_write_micros: optMicros(f.price_cw),
    extra: JSON.parse(f.extra || '{}'),
  })

  const save = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    try {
      const body = payload(form)
      if (editing === 'new') await api('/console/api/v1/models', { method: 'POST', body })
      else if (editing) await api(`/console/api/v1/models/${editing.id}`, { method: 'PUT', body })
      setEditing(null)
      models.reload()
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof SyntaxError ? 'Extra must be valid JSON.' : (err as Error).message)
    }
  }

  const toggle = async (m: Model) => {
    await api(`/console/api/v1/models/${m.id}`, { method: 'PUT', body: payload({ ...toForm(m), enabled: !m.enabled }) }).catch((e: Error) => alert(e.message))
    models.reload()
  }

  const remove = async (m: Model) => {
    if (!confirm(`Delete model "${m.name}"?`)) return
    await api(`/console/api/v1/models/${m.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }).catch((e: Error) => alert(e.message))
    models.reload()
  }

  const credName = (id: string | null) => (id ? creds.data?.find((c) => c.id === id)?.name ?? '…' : '—')
  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="Models">{writable && <Button onClick={() => open('new')}>Add model</Button>}</PageHeader>
      <ErrorText>{models.error}</ErrorText>
      <Table rows={models.data ?? []} cols={[
        { head: 'Name', cell: (m) => <span className="font-medium">{m.name}</span> },
        { head: 'Provider', cell: (m) => `${m.provider} · ${m.upstream_model}` },
        { head: '$ / 1M in · out', cell: (m) => `${fromMicros(m.price_input_micros)} · ${fromMicros(m.price_output_micros)}` },
        ...(writable ? [{ head: 'Credential', cell: (m: Model) => credName(m.credential_id) }] : []),
        { head: 'Status', cell: (m) => (m.enabled ? <Badge tone="green">enabled</Badge> : <Badge>disabled</Badge>) },
        { head: '', cell: (m) => writable && (
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => open(m)}>Edit</Button>
            <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => toggle(m)}>{m.enabled ? 'Disable' : 'Enable'}</Button>
            <Button variant="danger" onClick={() => remove(m)}>Delete</Button>
          </div>
        ) },
      ]} />

      {editing && (
        <Modal title={editing === 'new' ? 'Add model' : `Edit ${editing.name}`} onClose={() => setEditing(null)}>
          <form onSubmit={save} className="space-y-3">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <Field label="Name" hint="What clients send as model"><input className={inputCls} required value={form.name} onChange={set('name')} /></Field>
              <Field label="Provider">
                <select className={inputCls} value={form.provider} onChange={set('provider')}>
                  <option value="openai">OpenAI-compatible</option>
                  <option value="anthropic">Anthropic</option>
                  <option value="google">Google</option>
                </select>
              </Field>
              <Field label="Upstream model ID"><input className={inputCls} required value={form.upstream_model} onChange={set('upstream_model')} /></Field>
              <Field label="Credential">
                <select className={inputCls} value={form.credential_id} onChange={set('credential_id')}>
                  <option value="">None</option>
                  {creds.data?.map((c) => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.name} ({c.kind})</option>)}
                </select>
              </Field>
            </div>
            <Field label="API base" hint="Optional. Self-hosted, Azure, or Vertex endpoint override."><input className={inputCls} value={form.api_base} onChange={set('api_base')} /></Field>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <Field label="Input $ / 1M"><input className={inputCls} type="number" min="0" step="any" value={form.price_in} onChange={set('price_in')} /></Field>
              <Field label="Output $ / 1M"><input className={inputCls} type="number" min="0" step="any" value={form.price_out} onChange={set('price_out')} /></Field>
              <Field label="Cache read $ / 1M" hint="Blank = input price"><input className={inputCls} type="number" min="0" step="any" value={form.price_cr} onChange={set('price_cr')} /></Field>
              <Field label="Cache write $ / 1M" hint="Blank = input price"><input className={inputCls} type="number" min="0" step="any" value={form.price_cw} onChange={set('price_cw')} /></Field>
            </div>
            <Field label="Extra (JSON)" hint='e.g. {"vertex_project":"p","vertex_location":"us-east5","headers":{"X-Org":"me"}}'>
              <textarea className={inputCls + ' h-24 font-mono text-xs'} value={form.extra} onChange={set('extra')} />
            </Field>
            <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm"><input type="checkbox" checked={form.enabled} onChange={set('enabled')} /> Enabled</label>
            <ErrorText>{error}</ErrorText>
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
              <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>Cancel</Button>
              <Button>Save</Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </>
  )
}
```

`web/src/pages/Credentials.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { api, when } from '../api'
import { Button, ErrorText, Field, inputCls, Modal, PageHeader, Table, useLoad } from '../components/ui'
import type { Credential } from '../types'

export default function Credentials() {
  const creds = useLoad<Credential[]>('/console/api/v1/credentials')
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ name: '', kind: 'api_key', secret: '' })
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const save = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    try {
      await api('/console/api/v1/credentials', { method: 'POST', body: form })
      setOpen(false)
      setForm({ name: '', kind: 'api_key', secret: '' })
      creds.reload()
    } catch (err) {
      setError((err as Error).message)
    }
  }

  const remove = async (c: Credential) => {
    if (!confirm(`Delete credential "${c.name}"?`)) return
    await api(`/console/api/v1/credentials/${c.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }).catch((e: Error) =>
      alert(e.message.includes('in use') || e.message.includes('exists') ? 'Models still use this credential. Repoint them first.' : e.message))
    creds.reload()
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="Provider credentials"><Button onClick={() => { setOpen(true); setError('') }}>Add credential</Button></PageHeader>
      <p className="mb-4 text-sm text-zinc-600">Secrets are encrypted at rest and can't be viewed again. To rotate, add a new credential and repoint models.</p>
      <Table rows={creds.data ?? []} cols={[
        { head: 'Name', cell: (c) => <span className="font-medium">{c.name}</span> },
        { head: 'Kind', cell: (c) => c.kind },
        { head: 'Created', cell: (c) => when(c.created_at) },
        { head: '', cell: (c) => <Button variant="danger" onClick={() => remove(c)}>Delete</Button> },
      ]} />
      {open && (
        <Modal title="Add credential" onClose={() => setOpen(false)}>
          <form onSubmit={save} className="space-y-3">
            <Field label="Name"><input className={inputCls} required value={form.name} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })} /></Field>
            <Field label="Kind">
              <select className={inputCls} value={form.kind} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, kind: e.target.value })}>
                <option value="api_key">API key</option>
                <option value="gcp_service_account">GCP service account (Vertex)</option>
              </select>
            </Field>
            <Field label={form.kind === 'api_key' ? 'API key' : 'Service account JSON'}>
              {form.kind === 'api_key'
                ? <input className={inputCls} type="password" autoComplete="off" required value={form.secret} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, secret: e.target.value })} />
                : <textarea className={inputCls + ' h-32 font-mono text-xs'} required value={form.secret} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, secret: e.target.value })} />}
            </Field>
            <ErrorText>{error}</ErrorText>
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
              <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Cancel</Button>
              <Button>Save</Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </>
  )
}
```

`web/src/pages/Users.tsx`:
```tsx
import { api, when } from '../api'
import { useAuth } from '../auth'
import { Badge, Button, ErrorText, inputCls, PageHeader, Table, useLoad } from '../components/ui'
import type { Group, User } from '../types'

export default function Users() {
  const { me, can } = useAuth()
  const users = useLoad<User[]>('/console/api/v1/users')
  const groups = useLoad<Group[]>('/console/api/v1/groups')
  const writable = can('users:write')

  const update = async (u: User, patch: Partial<Pick<User, 'role' | 'group_id' | 'disabled'>>) => {
    await api(`/console/api/v1/users/${u.id}`, { method: 'PUT', body: patch }).catch((e: Error) => alert(e.message))
    users.reload()
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="Users" />
      <p className="mb-4 text-sm text-zinc-600">New users join through invite links (see Invites).</p>
      <ErrorText>{users.error}</ErrorText>
      <Table rows={users.data ?? []} cols={[
        { head: 'Name', cell: (u) => <span className="font-medium">{u.name}</span> },
        { head: 'Email', cell: (u) => u.email },
        { head: 'Role', cell: (u) => writable && u.id !== me?.user.id ? (
          <select aria-label={`Role for ${u.email}`} className={inputCls + ' mt-0 py-1'} value={u.role} onChange={(e) => update(u, { role: e.target.value as User['role'] })}>
            <option value="admin">admin</option><option value="member">member</option><option value="viewer">viewer</option>
          </select>
        ) : u.role },
        { head: 'Group', cell: (u) => writable ? (
          <select aria-label={`Group for ${u.email}`} className={inputCls + ' mt-0 py-1'} value={u.group_id} onChange={(e) => update(u, { group_id: e.target.value })}>
            {groups.data?.map((g) => <option key={g.id} value={g.id}>{g.name}</option>)}
          </select>
        ) : groups.data?.find((g) => g.id === u.group_id)?.name },
        { head: 'Joined', cell: (u) => when(u.created_at) },
        { head: 'Status', cell: (u) => (u.disabled ? <Badge tone="red">disabled</Badge> : <Badge tone="green">active</Badge>) },
        { head: '', cell: (u) => writable && u.id !== me?.user.id && (
          <Button variant={u.disabled ? 'ghost' : 'danger'} onClick={() => update(u, { disabled: !u.disabled })}>{u.disabled ? 'Enable' : 'Disable'}</Button>
        ) },
      ]} />
    </>
  )
}
```

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { api } from '../api'
import { useAuth } from '../auth'
import { budgetText, LimitsFields, limitsPayload, limitsState, type LimitsState } from '../components/LimitsFields'
import { Button, ErrorText, Field, inputCls, Modal, PageHeader, Table, useLoad } from '../components/ui'
import type { Group } from '../types'

export default function Groups() {
  const { can } = useAuth()
  const writable = can('groups:write')
  const groups = useLoad<Group[]>('/console/api/v1/groups')
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState<Group | 'new' | null>(null)
  const [name, setName] = useState('')
  const [limits, setLimits] = useState<LimitsState>(limitsState())
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const open = (g: Group | 'new') => {
    setEditing(g)
    setName(g === 'new' ? '' : g.name)
    setLimits(limitsState(g === 'new' ? undefined : g))
    setError('')
  }

  const save = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    const body = { name, ...limitsPayload(limits) }
    try {
      if (editing === 'new') await api('/console/api/v1/groups', { method: 'POST', body })
      else if (editing) await api(`/console/api/v1/groups/${editing.id}`, { method: 'PUT', body })
      setEditing(null)
      groups.reload()
    } catch (err) {
      setError((err as Error).message)
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="Groups">{writable && <Button onClick={() => open('new')}>New group</Button>}</PageHeader>
      <p className="mb-4 text-sm text-zinc-600">A group is a cost center. Its budget and rate limits apply on top of each key's own limits.</p>
      <ErrorText>{groups.error}</ErrorText>
      <Table rows={groups.data ?? []} cols={[
        { head: 'Name', cell: (g) => <span className="font-medium">{g.name}</span> },
        { head: 'Budget (USD)', cell: (g) => budgetText(g) },
        { head: 'RPM / TPM', cell: (g) => `${g.rpm_limit ?? '∞'} / ${g.tpm_limit ?? '∞'}` },
        { head: '', cell: (g) => writable && <Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => open(g)}>Edit</Button> },
      ]} />
      {editing && (
        <Modal title={editing === 'new' ? 'New group' : `Edit ${editing.name}`} onClose={() => setEditing(null)}>
          <form onSubmit={save} className="space-y-4">
            <Field label="Name"><input className={inputCls} required value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} /></Field>
            <LimitsFields value={limits} onChange={setLimits} />
            <ErrorText>{error}</ErrorText>
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
              <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>Cancel</Button>
              <Button>Save</Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </>
  )
}

`web/src/pages/Invites.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { api, when } from '../api'
import { Badge, Button, CopyBox, ErrorText, Field, inputCls, Modal, PageHeader, Table, useLoad } from '../components/ui'
import type { Group, Invite, User } from '../types'

const tone = { active: 'green', used: 'zinc', revoked: 'red', expired: 'amber' } as const

export default function Invites() {
  const invites = useLoad<Invite[]>('/console/api/v1/invites')
  const groups = useLoad<Group[]>('/console/api/v1/groups')
  const users = useLoad<User[]>('/console/api/v1/users')
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ role: 'member', group_id: '', email: '', days: '7' })
  const [url, setUrl] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')

  const create = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    try {
      const res = await api<{ url: string }>('/console/api/v1/invites', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: { role: form.role, group_id: form.group_id || groups.data?.[0]?.id, email: form.email, ttl_hours: Number(form.days) * 24 },
      })
      setOpen(false)
      setUrl(res.url)
      invites.reload()
    } catch (err) {
      setError((err as Error).message)
    }
  }

  const revoke = async (i: Invite) => {
    await api(`/console/api/v1/invites/${i.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }).catch((e: Error) => alert(e.message))
    invites.reload()
  }

  const set = (k: keyof typeof form) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLSelectElement>) => setForm({ ...form, [k]: e.target.value })
  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="Invites"><Button onClick={() => { setOpen(true); setError('') }}>Create invite</Button></PageHeader>
      <Table rows={invites.data ?? []} cols={[
        { head: 'For', cell: (i) => i.email || 'anyone with the link' },
        { head: 'Role', cell: (i) => i.role },
        { head: 'Group', cell: (i) => groups.data?.find((g) => g.id === i.group_id)?.name },
        { head: 'Created by', cell: (i) => users.data?.find((u) => u.id === i.created_by)?.email },
        { head: 'Expires', cell: (i) => when(i.expires_at) },
        { head: 'Status', cell: (i) => <Badge tone={tone[i.status]}>{i.status}</Badge> },
        { head: '', cell: (i) => i.status === 'active' && <Button variant="danger" onClick={() => revoke(i)}>Revoke</Button> },
      ]} />
      {open && (
        <Modal title="Create invite" onClose={() => setOpen(false)}>
          <form onSubmit={create} className="space-y-3">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
              <Field label="Role">
                <select className={inputCls} value={form.role} onChange={set('role')}>
                  <option value="member">member</option><option value="viewer">viewer</option><option value="admin">admin</option>
                </select>
              </Field>
              <Field label="Group">
                <select className={inputCls} value={form.group_id} onChange={set('group_id')}>
                  {groups.data?.map((g) => <option key={g.id} value={g.id}>{g.name}</option>)}
                </select>
              </Field>
            </div>
            <Field label="Restrict to email" hint="Optional"><input className={inputCls} type="email" value={form.email} onChange={set('email')} /></Field>
            <Field label="Valid for (days)"><input className={inputCls} type="number" min="1" max="30" value={form.days} onChange={set('days')} /></Field>
            <ErrorText>{error}</ErrorText>
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
              <Button type="button" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>Cancel</Button>
              <Button>Create</Button>
            </div>
          </form>
        </Modal>
      )}
      {url && (
        <Modal title="Invite link" onClose={() => setUrl('')}>
          <p className="mb-3 text-sm text-zinc-600">Send this link to the invitee. It works once.</p>
          <CopyBox value={url} />
          <div className="mt-4 flex justify-end"><Button onClick={() => setUrl('')}>Done</Button></div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { when } from '../api'
import { Button, ErrorText, inputCls, PageHeader, Table, useLoad } from '../components/ui'
import type { AuditEntry } from '../types'

const PAGE = 50

export default function Audit() {
  const [action, setAction] = useState('')
  const [offset, setOffset] = useState(0)
  const q = new URLSearchParams({ limit: String(PAGE), offset: String(offset), ...(action ? { action } : {}) })
  const audit = useLoad<{ entries: AuditEntry[]; total: number }>(`/console/api/v1/audit?${q}`)
  const total = audit.data?.total ?? 0

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="Audit log">
        <input aria-label="Filter by action" placeholder="Filter by action, e.g. key.create" className={inputCls + ' mt-0 w-64'}
          value={action} onChange={(e) => { setAction(e.target.value); setOffset(0) }} />
      </PageHeader>
      <ErrorText>{audit.error}</ErrorText>
      <Table rows={audit.data?.entries ?? []} cols={[
        { head: 'When', cell: (a) => when(a.created_at) },
        { head: 'Who', cell: (a) => a.actor_email || '—' },
        { head: 'Action', cell: (a) => <code className="text-xs">{a.action}</code> },
        { head: 'Resource', cell: (a) => `${a.resource} ${a.resource_id.slice(0, 8)}` },
        { head: 'IP', cell: (a) => a.ip },
        { head: 'Details', cell: (a) => (
          <details><summary className="cursor-pointer text-xs text-zinc-500">show</summary>
            <pre className="max-w-xl overflow-x-auto whitespace-pre-wrap text-xs">{JSON.stringify(a.details, null, 2)}</pre>
          </details>
        ) },
      ]} />
      <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-zinc-600">
        <span>{total === 0 ? 'No entries' : `${offset + 1}–${Math.min(offset + PAGE, total)} of ${total}`}</span>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <Button variant="ghost" disabled={offset === 0} onClick={() => setOffset(Math.max(0, offset - PAGE))}>Previous</Button>
          <Button variant="ghost" disabled={offset + PAGE >= total} onClick={() => setOffset(offset + PAGE)}>Next</Button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  )
}
```

`web/src/pages/Settings.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { api } from '../api'
import { useAuth } from '../auth'
import { Button, ErrorText, Field, inputCls, PageHeader, useLoad } from '../components/ui'
import type { Settings as S } from '../types'

const zones = Intl.supportedValuesOf('timeZone')

export default function Settings() {
  const { can, refresh } = useAuth()
  const current = useLoad<S>('/console/api/v1/settings')
  const [form, setForm] = useState<S | null>(null)
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState('')
  const [error, setError] = useState('')
  useEffect(() => { if (current.data) setForm(current.data) }, [current.data])
  if (!form) return <ErrorText>{current.error}</ErrorText>

  const save = async (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setMsg('')
    setError('')
    try {
      await api('/console/api/v1/settings', { method: 'PUT', body: form })
      await refresh()
      setMsg('Saved.')
    } catch (err) {
      setError((err as Error).message)
    }
  }
  const set = (k: keyof S) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => setForm({ ...form, [k]: e.target.value })
  const ro = !can('settings:write')
  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title="Settings" />
      <form onSubmit={save} className="max-w-md space-y-4">
        <Field label="Server name"><input className={inputCls} required disabled={ro} value={form.server_name} onChange={set('server_name')} /></Field>
        <Field label="Icon" hint="An emoji or image URL"><input className={inputCls} disabled={ro} value={form.server_icon} onChange={set('server_icon')} /></Field>
        <Field label="Timezone" hint="Daily/monthly budgets reset at midnight in this timezone">
          <input className={inputCls} list="zones" required disabled={ro} value={form.timezone} onChange={set('timezone')} />
          <datalist id="zones">{zones.map((z) => <option key={z} value={z} />)}</datalist>
        </Field>
        <Field label="Currency label" hint="Display only; no conversion"><input className={inputCls} required disabled={ro} value={form.currency} onChange={set('currency')} /></Field>
        <ErrorText>{error}</ErrorText>
        {msg && <p className="text-sm text-green-700">{msg}</p>}
        {!ro && <Button>Save</Button>}
      </form>
    </>
  )
}
```

`web/src/App.tsx`:
```tsx
import { BrowserRouter, Navigate, Route, Routes } from 'react-router-dom'
import { AuthProvider, Gate, RequireAuth } from './auth'
import Layout from './Layout'
import Audit from './pages/Audit'
import Credentials from './pages/Credentials'
import Groups from './pages/Groups'
import Invite from './pages/Invite'
import Invites from './pages/Invites'
import Keys from './pages/Keys'
import Login from './pages/Login'
import Models from './pages/Models'
import Settings from './pages/Settings'
import Users from './pages/Users'

const pages = [
  { path: 'keys', perm: 'keys:read_own', el: <Keys /> },
  { path: 'models', perm: 'models:read', el: <Models /> },
  { path: 'groups', perm: 'groups:read_own', el: <Groups /> },
  { path: 'users', perm: 'users:read', el: <Users /> },
  { path: 'invites', perm: 'invites:read', el: <Invites /> },
  { path: 'credentials', perm: 'models:write', el: <Credentials /> },
  { path: 'audit', perm: 'audit:read', el: <Audit /> },
  { path: 'settings', perm: 'settings:read', el: <Settings /> },
]

export default function App() {
  return (
    <BrowserRouter>
      <AuthProvider>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/login" element={<Login />} />
          <Route path="/invite/:token" element={<Invite />} />
          <Route element={<RequireAuth><Layout /></RequireAuth>}>
            <Route index element={<Navigate to="/keys" replace />} />
            {pages.map((p) => <Route key={p.path} path={p.path} element={<Gate perm={p.perm}>{p.el}</Gate>} />)}
          </Route>
          <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/" replace />} />
        </Routes>
      </AuthProvider>
    </BrowserRouter>
  )
}
```
