`web/src/pages/Dashboard.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Bar, BarChart, CartesianGrid, Line, LineChart, ResponsiveContainer, Tooltip, XAxis, YAxis } from 'recharts'
import { usd, when } from '../api'
import { useAuth } from '../auth'
import { Badge, ErrorText, inputCls, PageHeader, Table, useLoad } from '../components/ui'
import type { SpendLog, SpendRow } from '../types'

type Spend = { rows: SpendRow[]; recent: SpendLog[] }

function Stat({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4">
      <div className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">{label}</div>
      <div className="mt-1 text-2xl font-semibold">{value}</div>
    </div>
  )
}

export default function Dashboard() {
  const { can } = useAuth()
  const [days, setDays] = useState(30)
  // Rounded to the minute so the URL (and thus useLoad) is stable across renders.
  const from = new Date(Math.floor((Date.now() - days * 864e5) / 6e4) * 6e4).toISOString()
  const byDay = useLoad<Spend>(`/console/api/v1/spend?group_by=day&from=${encodeURIComponent(from)}`)
  const byModel = useLoad<Spend>(`/console/api/v1/spend?group_by=model&from=${encodeURIComponent(from)}`)

  const daily = (byDay.data?.rows ?? []).map((r) => ({ day: r.bucket.slice(5), usd: r.cost_nanos / 1e9, requests: r.requests }))
  const models = [...(byModel.data?.rows ?? [])].sort((a, b) => b.cost_nanos - a.cost_nanos).slice(0, 8)
    .map((r) => ({ model: r.bucket, usd: r.cost_nanos / 1e9 }))
  const totals = (byDay.data?.rows ?? []).reduce(
    (t, r) => ({ cost: t.cost + r.cost_nanos, req: t.req + r.requests, tok: t.tok + r.input_tokens + r.output_tokens }),
    { cost: 0, req: 0, tok: 0 },
  )

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader title={can('spend:read_all') ? 'Usage — everyone' : 'Your usage'}>
        <select aria-label="Time range" className={inputCls + ' mt-0'} value={days} onChange={(e) => setDays(Number(e.target.value))}>
          <option value={7}>Last 7 days</option>
          <option value={30}>Last 30 days</option>
          <option value={90}>Last 90 days</option>
        </select>
      </PageHeader>
      <ErrorText>{byDay.error || byModel.error}</ErrorText>
      <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <Stat label="Spend" value={usd(totals.cost)} />
        <Stat label="Requests" value={totals.req.toLocaleString()} />
        <Stat label="Tokens" value={totals.tok.toLocaleString()} />
      </div>
      <div className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">
        <section className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4">
          <h2 className="mb-2 text-sm font-medium text-zinc-700">Spend per day (USD)</h2>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={240}>
            <LineChart data={daily}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e4e4e7" />
              <XAxis dataKey="day" fontSize={12} />
              <YAxis fontSize={12} />
              <Tooltip formatter={(v: number) => `$${v.toFixed(4)}`} />
              <Line type="monotone" dataKey="usd" stroke="#18181b" strokeWidth={2} dot={false} />
            </LineChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </section>
        <section className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-200 bg-white p-4">
          <h2 className="mb-2 text-sm font-medium text-zinc-700">Top models by spend (USD)</h2>
          <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={240}>
            <BarChart data={models} layout="vertical" margin={{ left: 40 }}>
              <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="#e4e4e7" />
              <XAxis type="number" fontSize={12} />
              <YAxis type="category" dataKey="model" fontSize={12} width={120} />
              <Tooltip formatter={(v: number) => `$${v.toFixed(4)}`} />
              <Bar dataKey="usd" fill="#18181b" />
            </BarChart>
          </ResponsiveContainer>
        </section>
      </div>
      <h2 className="mb-2 text-sm font-medium text-zinc-700">Recent requests</h2>
      <Table rows={byDay.data?.recent ?? []} empty="No requests in this period." cols={[
        { head: 'When', cell: (r) => when(r.created_at) },
        { head: 'Model', cell: (r) => r.model_name },
        { head: 'Tokens in / out', cell: (r) => `${r.input_tokens} / ${r.output_tokens}` },
        { head: 'Cost', cell: (r) => usd(r.cost_nanos) },
        { head: 'Latency', cell: (r) => `${r.latency_ms} ms` },
        { head: 'Status', cell: (r) => r.status === 'success' ? <Badge tone="green">ok</Badge> : <Badge tone="red" >{r.error_message.slice(0, 40) || r.http_status}</Badge> },
      ]} />
    </>
  )
}
```

## Phase 5 — Ship

### Task 20: README, final verification

**Files:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `README.md`**

````markdown
# Tollgate

Self-hosted LLM gateway: one OpenAI-compatible endpoint in front of OpenAI-compatible, Anthropic, and Google models, with per-key and per-group budgets, rate limits, spend tracking, an audit log, and a built-in web console.

## Run it

```bash
cp .env.example .env
sed -i '' "s/^ENCRYPTION_KEY=.*/ENCRYPTION_KEY=$(openssl rand -hex 32)/" .env   # Linux: sed -i
docker compose up -d --build
docker compose exec tollgate /tollgate admin create --email you@example.com --name You
```

Open `BASE_URL` (default `http://localhost:4000`), sign in, then:

> Over plain `http` on anything other than `localhost` (and in Safari even on localhost), browsers drop the `Secure` session cookie and login appears to do nothing. Set `COOKIE_INSECURE=true` in `.env` for local testing only; in production, serve over HTTPS and leave it `false`.

1. **Credentials** → add a provider API key (or a GCP service-account JSON for Vertex).
2. **Models** → add a model: the name clients will send, the provider's model ID, the credential, and prices in USD per 1M tokens.
3. **API keys** → create a key and copy it.

```bash
curl $BASE_URL/v1/chat/completions -H "Authorization: Bearer tg-..." -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d '{"model":"<model name>","messages":[{"role":"user","content":"hello"}]}'
```

Any OpenAI SDK works with `base_url=$BASE_URL/v1`.

## Production notes

- Put it behind a TLS-terminating reverse proxy and set `BASE_URL` to the public `https://` URL. Set `TRUSTED_PROXIES` to the proxy's CIDR so client IPs in the audit log and login throttling are real.
- Back up Postgres **and** `ENCRYPTION_KEY`. Losing the key means re-entering provider credentials; nothing else is affected.
- Single instance only: the model registry and spend buffer live in process.

## Limits (v1)

- Tool/function calling is passed through for OpenAI-compatible models only; Anthropic and Google models return 400 for requests with `tools`.
- No `/v1/messages`, `/v1/responses`, images, or audio endpoints.

## Develop

```bash
docker compose up -d postgres redis
export TOLLGATE_TEST_DATABASE_URL='postgres://tollgate:tollgate@localhost:5432/tollgate?sslmode=disable'
go test ./...
BASE_URL=http://localhost:5173 COOKIE_INSECURE=true go run ./cmd/tollgate &   # BASE_URL must match the page origin (CSRF check)
cd web && npm run dev    # console on :5173, proxied to the Go server on :4000
```
````

## Self-Review

- **Spec coverage:** Goals 1–2 → Tasks 7–10, 12; 3 → 14; 4 → 4, 5, 12, 15; 5 → 4, 15, 17; 6 → 3, 5, 16; 7 → 5, 6, 12; 8 → 16, 18; 9 → 5, 16, 19; 10 → 4, 16, 18; 11 → 15, 16; 12 → file map (core never imports console). Security table → 6 (throttle), 12 (key auth), 14 (CSRF, client IP, sessions), 3 (vault). Background jobs → 11, 13. Retention → 5, 13.
- **Type consistency:** `repo.Limits` (embedded in `Group`, `APIKey`) is what `api.admit` reads; `llm.Usage.Tokens()` drives TPM; `Server.Record` matches `spend.Recorder.Record`; console `Reload` matches `serve`'s `reload`.
